import { Component, OnInit, ChangeDetectionStrategy, signal } from '@angular/core';
import { CommonModule } from '@angular/common';
import { FormsModule } from '@angular/forms';
import { HttpErrorResponse } from '@angular/common/http';
import { CreditNoteService } from '../../core/services/credit-note.service';
import { CreditNoteDtoResponse } from '../../models/CreditNote-model'; // AsegÃºrate de importar tu interfaz correcta

import { BookingCancellationDtoResponse } from '../../models/Booking-model';
import { BookingService } from '../../core/services/booking.service';

@Component({
  selector: 'app-credit-note-list',
  standalone: true,
  imports: [CommonModule, FormsModule],
  templateUrl: './credit-note-list.component.html',
  styleUrls: ['./credit-note-list.component.css'],
  changeDetection: ChangeDetectionStrategy.OnPush
})
export class CreditNoteListComponent implements OnInit {
  // --- Estados con Signals ---
  creditNotes = signal<CreditNoteDtoResponse[]>([]);
  allCreditNotesCache = signal<CreditNoteDtoResponse[]>([]); // CachÃ© para bÃºsquedas
  loading = signal<boolean>(false);
  errorMessage = signal<string>('');
  searchCode = signal<string>('');

  // --- Estados del Modal de Detalle con Signals ---
  showModal = signal<boolean>(false);
  loadingDetail = signal<boolean>(false);
  cancellationDetail = signal<BookingCancellationDtoResponse | null>(null);
  detailError = signal<string>('');
  selectedCreditNote = signal<CreditNoteDtoResponse | null>(null);

  constructor(
    private creditNoteService: CreditNoteService,
    private bookingService: BookingService
  ) {}

  ngOnInit(): void {
    this.loadCreditNotes();
  }

  loadCreditNotes(): void {
    this.loading.set(true);
    this.errorMessage.set('');
    
    this.creditNoteService.getAllCreditNotes().subscribe({
      next: (notes) => {
        console.log("Notas de crÃ©dito cargadas del backend:", notes);
        const data = Array.isArray(notes) ? notes : [];
        this.creditNotes.set(data);
        this.allCreditNotesCache.set(data);
        this.loading.set(false);
      },
      error: (err: HttpErrorResponse) => {
        console.error('Error al cargar las notas de crÃ©dito:', err);
        this.errorMessage.set('No se pudieron cargar las notas de crÃ©dito.');
        this.loading.set(false);
      }
    });
  }

  onSearch(): void {
    const query = this.searchCode().trim().toLowerCase();
    const cache = this.allCreditNotesCache();

    if (!query) {
      this.creditNotes.set(cache);
      return;
    }
    
    const filtered = cache.filter(note => 
      String(note.id).includes(query) || 
      String(note.accountId).includes(query) ||
      String(note.clientName || '').toLowerCase().includes(query) ||
      String(note.clientLastName || '').toLowerCase().includes(query) ||
      String(note.username || '').toLowerCase().includes(query)
    );

    this.creditNotes.set(filtered);
  }

  // --- MÃ©todo para cambiar el estado de pago (paid) ---
  togglePaidStatus(id: number): void {
    this.creditNoteService.togglePaidStatus(id).subscribe({
      next: (updatedNote) => {
        console.log('Estado de pago actualizado para la nota:', updatedNote);

        const updateList = (list: CreditNoteDtoResponse[]) =>
          list.map(note => {
            if (note.id === id) {
              return {
                ...note,
                paid: updatedNote.paid
              };
            }
            return note;
          });

        this.allCreditNotesCache.update(updateList);
        this.creditNotes.update(updateList);
      },
      error: (err: HttpErrorResponse) => {
        console.error('Error al actualizar el estado de pago:', err);
        this.errorMessage.set('No se pudo actualizar el estado de pago de la nota de crÃ©dito.');
      }
    });
  }

  viewDetail(id: number): void {
    console.log('Ver detalle de nota de crÃ©dito ID:', id);
    const note = this.creditNotes().find(n => n.id === id);
    if (!note) return;

    this.selectedCreditNote.set(note);
    this.showModal.set(true);
    this.loadingDetail.set(true);
    this.cancellationDetail.set(null);
    this.detailError.set('');

    // Si tu nota de crÃ©dito trae vinculado el bookingId en el DTO o en la cuenta, lo utilizas aquÃ­.
    // Si usas el accountId para buscar la reserva, asegÃºrate de tener el mÃ©todo o id disponible.
    const targetAccountId = note.accountId;
    if (!targetAccountId) {
      this.loadingDetail.set(false);
      this.detailError.set('La nota de crÃ©dito no contiene un identificador de cuenta asociado.');
      return;
    }

    // Opcional: si necesitas buscar por cuenta o si manejas la relaciÃ³n en el historial
    this.bookingService.getCancellationHistory().subscribe({
      next: (history: BookingCancellationDtoResponse[]) => {
        // Buscamos en el historial (puedes ajustar el criterio si manejas accountId o bookingId)
        const found = history.find(c => c.bookingId === targetAccountId); // Ajustar si es bookingId
        if (found) {
          this.cancellationDetail.set(found);
        } else {
          this.detailError.set(`No se encontrÃ³ un registro de cancelaciÃ³n vinculado.`);
        }
        this.loadingDetail.set(false);
      },
      error: (err: HttpErrorResponse) => {
        console.error('Error al obtener el historial de cancelaciones:', err);
        this.loadingDetail.set(false);
        this.detailError.set('Error de conexiÃ³n al intentar obtener los detalles de la cancelaciÃ³n.');
      }
    });
  }

  closeModal(): void {
    this.showModal.set(false);
    this.selectedCreditNote.set(null);
    this.cancellationDetail.set(null);
    this.detailError.set('');
  }
}

              <label for="address">DirecciÃ³n</label>
              <input id="address" type="text" formControlName="address" class="form-control">
            </div>
          </div>
        </fieldset>

        <!-- DATOS LABORALES -->
        <fieldset class="employee-fieldset">
          <legend class="employee-legend">Datos Laborales</legend>

          <div class="form-grid">
            <div class="form-group">
              <label for="employeeNumber">NÃºmero de empleado</label>
              <input id="employeeNumber" type="text" formControlName="employeeNumber" class="form-control">
            </div>

            <div class="form-group">
              <label for="hireDate">Fecha de contrataciÃ³n *</label>
              <input id="hireDate" type="date" formControlName="hireDate" class="form-control">
            </div>

            <div class="form-group">
              <label for="shift">Turno *</label>
              <select id="shift" formControlName="shift" class="form-control">
                @for (shift of availableShifts; track shift) {
                  <option [value]="shift">{{ shift }}</option>
                }
              </select>
            </div>

            <div class="form-group">
              <label for="salary">Salario *</label>
              <input id="salary" type="number" step="0.01" min="0.01" formControlName="salary" class="form-control">
            </div>
          </div>
        </fieldset>

        <!-- FOTO DE PERFIL -->
        <fieldset class="employee-fieldset">
          <legend class="employee-legend">Foto de Perfil</legend>
          <div class="form-grid">
            <div class="form-group full-width">
              <label for="profilePictureFile">Seleccionar nueva imagen (Opcional)</label>
              <input type="file" id="profilePictureFile" class="form-control file-input" (change)="onFileSelected($event)" accept="image/*">
            </div>
          </div>
        </fieldset>

        <!-- Acciones del Formulario -->
        <div class="employee-modal-actions">
          <button type="button" class="btn-cancel" routerLink="/dashboard/employee-list">
            Cancelar
          </button>

          <button type="submit" class="btn-save" [disabled]="saving() || employeeForm.invalid">
            @if (saving()) {
              Guardando cambios...
            } @else {
              Guardar cambios
            }
          </button>
        </div>

      </form>

    </div>
  }

  <!-- Volver al listado cuando no se pudo cargar -->
  @if (!loading() && !employee()) {
    <div class="employee-edit-card text-center">
      <p class="error-message">No se pudo cargar la informaciÃ³n del empleado.</p>
      <button type="button" class="btn-detail" routerLink="/dashboard/employee-list">
        Volver al listado
      </button>
    </div>
  }

</div>
import { Component, inject, OnInit, signal } from '@angular/core';
import {
  ReactiveFormsModule,
  NonNullableFormBuilder,
  Validators
} from '@angular/forms';
import { ActivatedRoute, Router, RouterLink } from '@angular/router';
import { forkJoin } from 'rxjs'; // ðŸ‘ˆ Importante

import { EmployeeService } from '../../../core/services/employee.service';
import { UserService } from '../../../core/services/user.service'; // ðŸ‘ˆ AsegÃºrate de tenerlo inyectado

import {
  EmployeeDtoRequest,
  EmployeeDtoResponse,
  ShiftType
} from '../../../models/Employee-model';

@Component({
  selector: 'app-employee-edit',
  standalone: true,
  imports: [ReactiveFormsModule, RouterLink],
  templateUrl: './employee-edit.component.html',
  styleUrl: './employee-edit.component.css'
})
export class EmployeeEditComponent implements OnInit {

  private fb = inject(NonNullableFormBuilder);
  private employeeService = inject(EmployeeService);
  private userService = inject(UserService);
  private route = inject(ActivatedRoute);
  private router = inject(Router);

  employeeId = signal<number | null>(null);
  employee = signal<EmployeeDtoResponse | null>(null);
  selectedProfilePicture: File | null = null;

  loading = signal(false);
  saving = signal(false);
  errorMessage = signal('');
  successMessage = signal('');

  readonly availableShifts: ShiftType[] = [
    'MORNING',
    'AFTERNOON',
    'NIGHT'
  ];

  employeeForm = this.fb.group({
    username: ['', [Validators.required]],
    name: ['', [Validators.required]],
    surname: ['', [Validators.required]],
    dni: ['', [Validators.required]],
    gender: [''],
    email: ['', [Validators.required, Validators.email]],
    phoneNumber: [''],
    address: [''],
    birthDay: [''],

    employeeNumber: [''],
    hireDate: ['', [Validators.required]],
    shift: this.fb.control<ShiftType>('MORNING', [Validators.required]),
    salary: [0, [Validators.required, Validators.min(0.01)]]
  });

  ngOnInit(): void {
    const idParam = this.route.snapshot.paramMap.get('id');
    const id = Number(idParam);

    if (!idParam || !Number.isSafeInteger(id) || id <= 0) {
      this.errorMessage.set('El ID del empleado no es vÃ¡lido.');
      return;
    }

    this.employeeId.set(id);
    this.loadEmployee(id);
  }

  loadEmployee(id: number): void {
    this.loading.set(true);
    this.errorMessage.set('');

    // Consultamos empleado y usuario en paralelo ya que comparten el mismo ID
    forkJoin({
      employee: this.employeeService.getById(id),
      user: this.userService.getById(id)
    }).subscribe({
      next: ({ employee, user }: { employee: any, user: any }) => {
        this.employee.set(employee);

        this.employeeForm.patchValue({
          // Datos completos del usuario
          username: user.username || '',
          name: user.name || '',
          surname: user.surname || '',
          dni: user.dni || '',
          gender: user.gender || '',
          email: user.email || '',
          phoneNumber: user.phoneNumber || '',
          address: user.address || '',
          birthDay: user.birthDay || '',

          // Datos laborales del empleado
          employeeNumber: employee.employeeNumber || '',
          hireDate: employee.hireDate || '',
          shift: employee.shift || 'MORNING',
          salary: employee.salary || 0
        });

        this.loading.set(false);
      },
      error: (err) => {
        console.error('Error al cargar datos:', err);
        this.errorMessage.set('No se pudieron cargar los datos del empleado.');
        this.loading.set(false);
      }
    });
  }

  onFileSelected(event: any): void {
    const file: File = event.target.files[0];
    if (file) {
      this.selectedProfilePicture = file;
    }
  }

 onSubmit(): void {
    if (this.saving() || this.loading()) return;

    const id = this.employeeId();
    if (id === null) return;

    if (this.employeeForm.invalid) {
      this.employeeForm.markAllAsTouched();
      return;
    }

    const formValue = this.employeeForm.getRawValue();

    const updatePayload: EmployeeDtoRequest = {
      userId: id,
      username: formValue.username,
      name: formValue.name,
      surname: formValue.surname,
      dni: formValue.dni,
      gender: formValue.gender || undefined,
      email: formValue.email,
      phoneNumber: formValue.phoneNumber || undefined,
      address: formValue.address || undefined,
      birthDay: formValue.birthDay || undefined,
      employeeNumber: formValue.employeeNumber || undefined,
      hireDate: formValue.hireDate,
      shift: formValue.shift,
      salary: Number(formValue.salary)
    };

    this.saving.set(true);
    this.errorMessage.set('');
    this.successMessage.set('');

    this.employeeService.update(id, updatePayload).subscribe({
      next: () => {
        if (this.selectedProfilePicture) {
          this.uploadProfilePicture(id, this.selectedProfilePicture);
        } else {
          this.saving.set(false);
          this.successMessage.set('Empleado y usuario actualizados correctamente.');
          this.router.navigate(['/dashboard/employee-list']);
        }
      },
      error: (err) => {
        this.errorMessage.set(err.error?.message || 'No se pudo actualizar la informaciÃ³n.');
        this.saving.set(false);
      }
    });
  }

  uploadProfilePicture(userId: number, file: File): void {
    const formData = new FormData();
    formData.append('profilePicture', file);

    this.userService.updateProfilePicture(userId, formData).subscribe({
      next: () => {
        this.saving.set(false);
        this.successMessage.set('Empleado, usuario y foto actualizados correctamente.');
        this.router.navigate(['/dashboard/employee-list']);
      },
      error: () => {
        this.saving.set(false);
        this.successMessage.set('Se guardaron los datos, pero hubo un error al actualizar la foto.');
        this.router.navigate(['/dashboard/employee-list']);
      }
    });
  }
}

import { Component, inject, signal } from '@angular/core';
import {
  ReactiveFormsModule,
  NonNullableFormBuilder,
  Validators
} from '@angular/forms';
import { Router, RouterLink } from '@angular/router';

import { EmployeeService } from '../../../core/services/employee.service';

import {
  EmployeeCreateUnifiedDTO,
  RoleType,
  ShiftType
} from '../../../models/Employee-model';

@Component({
  selector: 'app-employee-form',
  standalone: true,
  imports: [ReactiveFormsModule, RouterLink],
  templateUrl: './employee-form.component.html',
  styleUrl: './employee-form.component.css'
})
export class EmployeeFormComponent {

  private fb = inject(NonNullableFormBuilder);
  private employeeService = inject(EmployeeService);
  private router = inject(Router);

  saving = signal(false);
  errorMessage = signal('');
  fileError = signal('');

  selectedFile = signal<File | null>(null);

  availableRoles: RoleType[] = [
    'ADMIN',
    'RECEPCIONIST',
    'HOUSEKEEPING',
    'MAINTENANCE',
    'RELIEF_STAFF'
  ];

  availableShifts: ShiftType[] = [
    'MORNING',
    'AFTERNOON',
    'NIGHT'
  ];

  availableGenders: string[] = [
    'MASCULINO',
    'FEMENINO',
    'OTRO',
    'PREFIERO NO DECIR'
  ];

  private getTodayDate(): string {
    const today = new Date();

    const year = today.getFullYear();
    const month = String(today.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, '0');
    const day = String(today.getDate()).padStart(2, '0');

    return `${year}-${month}-${day}`;
  }

  employeeForm = this.fb.group({

    // Datos del usuario
    username: ['', [
      Validators.required
    ]],

    password: ['', [
      Validators.required
    ]],

    name: ['', [
      Validators.required,
      Validators.minLength(3),
      Validators.maxLength(50)
    ]],

    surname: ['', [
      Validators.required,
      Validators.minLength(3),
      Validators.maxLength(50)
    ]],

    dni: ['', [
      Validators.required,
      Validators.pattern(/^\d{7,10}$/)
    ]],

    gender: ['MASCULINO', [
      Validators.maxLength(20)
    ]],

    email: ['', [
      Validators.required,
      Validators.email
    ]],

    phoneNumber: ['', [
      Validators.maxLength(15)
    ]],

    address: ['', [
      Validators.maxLength(150)
    ]],

    birthDay: ['', [
      Validators.required
    ]],

    role: this.fb.control<RoleType>('RECEPCIONIST', [
      Validators.required
    ]),

    // Datos laborales
    employeeNumber: [''],

    emergencyPhoneNumber: [''],

    hireDate: [this.getTodayDate()],

    shift: this.fb.control<ShiftType>('MORNING'),

    salary: [0]

  });

  // Capturar y validar fotografÃ­a
  onFileSelected(event: Event): void {

    const input = event.target as HTMLInputElement;
    const file = input.files?.[0];

    this.fileError.set('');
    this.selectedFile.set(null);

    if (!file) {
      return;
    }

    const maxSizeInBytes = 2 * 1024 * 1024;

    if (file.size > maxSizeInBytes) {
      const fileSizeMb = (file.size / (1024 * 1024)).toFixed(1);

      this.fileError.set(`La imagen pesa ${fileSizeMb} MB. El tamaÃ±o mÃ¡ximo permitido es de 2 MB.`);

      input.value = '';
      return;
    }

    this.selectedFile.set(file);
  }

  // Guardar empleado
  onSubmit(): void {

    if (this.employeeForm.invalid) {
      this.employeeForm.markAllAsTouched();
      return;
    }

    if (this.fileError()) {
      return;
    }

    if (this.saving()) {
      return;
    }

    const formValue = this.employeeForm.getRawValue();

    // La fecha de nacimiento debe ser anterior a hoy
    if (formValue.birthDay >= this.getTodayDate()) {
      this.errorMessage.set('La fecha de nacimiento debe ser anterior a hoy.');
      return;
    }

    this.saving.set(true);
    this.errorMessage.set('');

    const employee: EmployeeCreateUnifiedDTO = {

      // Datos del usuario
      username: formValue.username,
      password: formValue.password,
      name: formValue.name,
      surname: formValue.surname,
      dni: formValue.dni,

      gender: formValue.gender || undefined,

      email: formValue.email,

      phoneNumber: formValue.phoneNumber || undefined,
      address: formValue.address || undefined,

      birthDay: formValue.birthDay,
      role: formValue.role,

      profilePictureFile: this.selectedFile() || undefined,

      // Datos laborales
      employeeNumber: formValue.employeeNumber || undefined,

      emergencyPhoneNumber:
        formValue.emergencyPhoneNumber || undefined,

      hireDate: formValue.hireDate || this.getTodayDate(),

      shift: formValue.shift,
      salary: Number(formValue.salary)
    };

    this.employeeService.create(employee).subscribe({
      next: () => {
        this.saving.set(false);
        this.router.navigate(['/dashboard/employee-list']);
      },

      error: () => {
        this.errorMessage.set('No se pudo registrar el empleado. VerificÃ¡ que el DNI, email y nombre de usuario no estÃ©n registrados.');
        this.saving.set(false);
      }
    });
  }
}

        <!-- InformaciÃ³n del empleado -->

        <!-- Mensaje de error -->
        @if (shiftError()) {
          <p class="error-message" role="alert">
            {{ shiftError() }}
          </p>
        }

        <!-- Formulario reactivo -->
        <form
          [formGroup]="shiftForm"
          (ngSubmit)="saveShiftChange()">

          <fieldset [disabled]="updatingShift()">

            <legend>Nuevo turno</legend>

            <div>

              <label for="newShift">

              <select

                    {{ shift }}
                  </option>
                }

              </select>

              @if (
                shiftForm.controls.shift.touched &&
                shiftForm.controls.shift.hasError('required')
              ) {
                <small class="error-message">
                  Debe seleccionar un turno.
                </small>
              }

              @if (
                shiftForm.controls.shift.value === employee.shift
              ) {
                <p>
                  Seleccione un turno diferente al actual.
                </p>
              }

            </div>

          </fieldset>

          <!-- Acciones del formulario -->
          <div class="employee-modal-actions">

            <button
              type="button"
              class="btn-cancel"
              [disabled]="updatingShift()"
              (click)="cancelShiftChange()">
              Cancelar
            </button>

            <button
              type="submit"
              class="btn-save"
              [disabled]="
                updatingShift() ||
                shiftForm.invalid ||
                shiftForm.controls.shift.value === employee.shift
              ">

              @if (updatingShift()) {
                Actualizando turno...
              } @else {
                Confirmar cambio
              }

            </button>

          </div>

        </form>

      </section>

    </div>

  }

</div>
import { Component, inject, OnInit, OnDestroy, signal } from '@angular/core';
import { DecimalPipe } from '@angular/common';
import { RouterLink } from '@angular/router';
import {
  ReactiveFormsModule,
  NonNullableFormBuilder,
  Validators
} from '@angular/forms';

import { EmployeeService } from '../../../core/services/employee.service';
import { UserService } from '../../../core/services/user.service';

import { EmployeeDtoResponse, ShiftType } from '../../../models/Employee-model';
import { UserDtoResponse } from '../../../models/User-model';

@Component({
  selector: 'app-employee-list',
  standalone: true,
  imports: [RouterLink, DecimalPipe, ReactiveFormsModule],
  templateUrl: './employee-list.component.html',
  styleUrl: './employee-list.component.css'
})
export class EmployeeListComponent implements OnInit, OnDestroy {

  private employeeService = inject(EmployeeService);
  private userService = inject(UserService);
  private fb = inject(NonNullableFormBuilder);

  employees = signal<EmployeeDtoResponse[]>([]);
  loading = signal(false);
  errorMessage = signal('');

  isAdmin = signal(false);

  selectedEmployee = signal<EmployeeDtoResponse | null>(null);
  selectedUser = signal<UserDtoResponse | null>(null);

  detailLoading = signal(false);
  detailError = signal('');

  employeeAvatars = signal<Record<number, string>>({});
  employeeRoles = signal<Record<number, string>>({});

  deletingId = signal<number | null>(null);
  defaultAvatar = 'assets/default-avatar.png';

  selectedEmployeeForShift = signal<EmployeeDtoResponse | null>(null);
  updatingShift = signal(false);
  shiftError = signal('');

  readonly availableShifts: ShiftType[] = [
    'MORNING',
    'AFTERNOON',
    'NIGHT'
  ];

  shiftForm = this.fb.group({
    shift: this.fb.control<ShiftType>('MORNING', [
      Validators.required
    ])
  });

  ngOnInit(): void {
    this.checkUserRole();
    this.loadEmployees();
  }

  checkUserRole(): void {
    const storedRoles = localStorage.getItem('role');

    if (!storedRoles) {
      this.isAdmin.set(false);
      return;
    }

    try {
      const roles = JSON.parse(storedRoles); 
      const roleList = Array.isArray(roles) ? roles : [roles];

      const admin = roleList.some((role: unknown) => {
        if (typeof role === 'string') {
          return role === 'ADMIN' || role === 'ROLE_ADMIN';
        }

        if (typeof role === 'object' && role !== null) {
          const data = role as Record<string, unknown>;
          return (
            data['authority'] === 'ADMIN' ||
            data['authority'] === 'ROLE_ADMIN' ||
            data['name'] === 'ADMIN' ||
            data['name'] === 'ROLE_ADMIN'
          );
        }

        return false;
      });

      this.isAdmin.set(admin);

    } catch {
      this.isAdmin.set(
        storedRoles === 'ADMIN' || storedRoles === 'ROLE_ADMIN'
      );
    }
  }

  loadEmployees(): void {
    this.loading.set(true);
    this.errorMessage.set('');

    this.employeeService.getAll().subscribe({
      next: (data) => {
        const employees = Array.isArray(data) ? data : [];

        this.employees.set(employees);
        this.loading.set(false);

        employees.forEach(employee => {
          this.loadEmployeeAvatar(employee.id); 
          this.loadEmployeeRole(employee.id);
        });
      },
      error: () => {
        this.errorMessage.set('No se pudieron cargar los empleados o no tienes permisos.');
        this.loading.set(false);
      }
    });
  }

  private loadEmployeeRole(employeeId: number): void {
    this.userService.getById(employeeId).subscribe({
      next: (user: any) => {
        const roleNames = user.roles.map((role: any) => role.name);
        const roles = roleNames.join(', ') || 'N/A';

        this.employeeRoles.update(currentRoles => ({
          ...currentRoles,
          [employeeId]: roles
        }));
      },
      error: () => {
        this.employeeRoles.update(currentRoles => ({
          ...currentRoles,
          [employeeId]: 'N/A'
        }));
      }
    });
  }

  getEmployeeRole(employeeId: number): string {
    return this.employeeRoles()[employeeId] || 'Cargando...';
  }

  private loadEmployeeAvatar(employeeId: number): void {
    if (this.employeeAvatars()[employeeId]) {
      return;
    }

    this.userService.getProfilePicture(employeeId).subscribe({
      next: (blob) => {
        const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
        this.employeeAvatars.update(avatars => ({
          ...avatars,
          [employeeId]: url
        }));
      },
      error: () => {
        this.employeeAvatars.update(avatars => ({
          ...avatars,
          [employeeId]: this.defaultAvatar
        }));
      }
    });
  }

  getEmployeeAvatarUrl(employeeId: number): string {
    return this.employeeAvatars()[employeeId] || this.defaultAvatar;
  }

  openEmployeeDetail(employee: EmployeeDtoResponse): void {
    this.selectedEmployee.set(employee);
    this.selectedUser.set(null);

    this.detailLoading.set(true);
    this.detailError.set('');

    this.userService.getById(employee.id).subscribe({
      next: (user) => {
        if (this.selectedEmployee()?.id !== employee.id) {
          return;
        }

        this.selectedUser.set(user);
        this.detailLoading.set(false);
      },
      error: () => {
        if (this.selectedEmployee()?.id !== employee.id) {
          return;
        }
        this.detailError.set('No se pudo cargar la informaciÃ³n del usuario asociado.');
        this.detailLoading.set(false);
      }
    });
  }

  closeEmployeeDetail(): void {
    this.selectedEmployee.set(null);
    this.selectedUser.set(null);
    this.detailError.set('');
    this.detailLoading.set(false);
  }

  openShiftForm(employee: EmployeeDtoResponse): void {
    if (!this.isAdmin() || this.updatingShift()) {
      return;
    }

    this.selectedEmployeeForShift.set(employee);
    this.shiftError.set('');

    this.shiftForm.reset({
      shift: employee.shift
    });
  }

  cancelShiftChange(): void {
    if (this.updatingShift()) {
      return;
    }

    this.selectedEmployeeForShift.set(null);
    this.shiftError.set('');
    this.shiftForm.reset();
  }

  saveShiftChange(): void {
    if (!this.isAdmin()) {
      this.shiftError.set('No tienes permisos para cambiar el turno.');
      return;
    }

    const employee = this.selectedEmployeeForShift();

    if (!employee || this.updatingShift()) {
      return;
    }

    if (this.shiftForm.invalid) {
      this.shiftForm.markAllAsTouched();
      return;
    }

    const nuevoShift = this.shiftForm.controls.shift.value;

    if (nuevoShift === employee.shift) {
      this.shiftError.set('Debe seleccionar un turno diferente al actual.');
      return;
    }

    this.updatingShift.set(true);
    this.shiftError.set('');

    this.employeeService.updateShift(employee.id, nuevoShift).subscribe({
      next: (updatedEmployee) => {
        this.employees.update(employees =>
          employees.map(current =>
            current.id === updatedEmployee.id ? updatedEmployee : current
          )
        );

        if (this.selectedEmployee()?.id === updatedEmployee.id) {
          this.selectedEmployee.set(updatedEmployee);
        }

        this.updatingShift.set(false);
        this.selectedEmployeeForShift.set(null);
        this.shiftForm.reset();
      },
      error: () => {
        this.shiftError.set('No se pudo actualizar el turno del empleado.');
        this.updatingShift.set(false);
      }
    });
  }

  deleteEmployee(id: number): void {
    if (!this.isAdmin()) {
      this.errorMessage.set('No tienes permisos para eliminar empleados.');
      return;
    }

    if (this.deletingId() !== null) {
      return;
    }

    this.deletingId.set(id);
    this.errorMessage.set('');

    this.employeeService.delete(id).subscribe({
      next: () => {
        this.employees.update(employees =>
          employees.filter(employee => employee.id !== id)
        );

        const avatar = this.employeeAvatars()[id];

        if (avatar?.startsWith('blob:')) {
          URL.revokeObjectURL(avatar);
        }

        this.employeeAvatars.update(avatars => {
          const updated = { ...avatars };
          delete updated[id];
          return updated;
        });

        this.employeeRoles.update(roles => {
          const updated = { ...roles };
          delete updated[id];
          return updated;
        });

        if (this.selectedEmployee()?.id === id) {
          this.closeEmployeeDetail();
        }

        if (this.selectedEmployeeForShift()?.id === id) {
          this.cancelShiftChange();
        }

        this.deletingId.set(null);
      },
      error: () => {
        this.errorMessage.set('No se pudo eliminar el empleado.');
        this.deletingId.set(null);
      }
    });
  }

  ngOnDestroy(): void {
    Object.values(this.employeeAvatars()).forEach(url => {
      if (url.startsWith('blob:')) {
        URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
      }
    });
  }
}

import { Component, OnInit, inject, signal } from '@angular/core';
import { CommonModule } from '@angular/common';

import { RoomService } from '../../core/services/room.service';
import { BookingService } from '../../core/services/booking.service';
import { PaymentService } from '../../core/services/payment.service';
import { RoomAttentionService } from '../../core/services/room-attention.service';

@Component({
  selector: 'app-inicio',
  standalone: true,
  imports: [CommonModule],
  templateUrl: './inicio.component.html',
  styleUrl: './inicio.component.css'
})
export class InicioComponent implements OnInit {

  private roomService = inject(RoomService);
  private bookingService = inject(BookingService);
  private paymentService = inject(PaymentService);
  private attentionService = inject(RoomAttentionService);

  // KPIs principales
  totalRooms = signal<number>(0);
  activeBookingsCount = signal<number>(0);     // EstadÃ­as activas
  pendingBookingsCount = signal<number>(0);    // NUEVO: Reservas pendientes
  todayRevenue = signal<number>(0);            // Ingresos del dÃ­a corregidos
  pendingAttentionsCount = signal<number>(0);  // Servicios pendientes

  // Variables para el grÃ¡fico de torta de ocupaciÃ³n
  occupancyPercentage = signal<number>(0);
  occupiedRoomsCount = signal<number>(0);
  availableRoomsCount = signal<number>(0);

  ngOnInit(): void {
    this.cargarDatosDashboard();
  }

  cargarDatosDashboard(): void {
    // 1. Total de habitaciones, estadÃ­as activas, reservas pendientes y grÃ¡fico
    this.roomService.getRoomCount().subscribe({
      next: (totalCount) => {
        this.totalRooms.set(totalCount);

        this.bookingService.getAll().subscribe({
          next: (bookings) => {
            // EstadÃ­as activas (ej. CHECKED_IN o CONFIRMED segÃºn tu negocio)
            const activas = bookings.filter(b => b.state === 'CHECKED_IN' || b.state === 'CONFIRMED');
            const ocupadasCount = activas.length;
            this.activeBookingsCount.set(ocupadasCount);
            this.occupiedRoomsCount.set(ocupadasCount);

            // NUEVO: Reservas pendientes (ej. estado 'PENDING')
            const pendientesReservas = bookings.filter(b => b.state === 'PENDING');
            this.pendingBookingsCount.set(pendientesReservas.length);

            // Habitaciones disponibles
            const disponibles = Math.max(0, totalCount - ocupadasCount);
            this.availableRoomsCount.set(disponibles);

            // Porcentaje de ocupaciÃ³n
            if (totalCount > 0) {
              const porcentaje = (ocupadasCount / totalCount) * 100;
              this.occupancyPercentage.set(Math.round(porcentaje));
            }
          },
          error: (err) => console.error('Error al cargar reservas', err)
        });
      },
      error: (err) => console.error('Error al cargar habitaciones', err)
    });

    // 2. Ingresos del DÃ­a (Robusto y con diagnÃ³stico en consola)
    this.paymentService.getAllPayments().subscribe({
      next: (payments) => {
        // Imprimimos en consola los pagos para ver exactamente cÃ³mo llega 'paymentDate'
        console.log('Pagos obtenidos del servidor:', payments);

        const hoy = new Date();
        
        const sumaHoy = payments
          .filter(p => {
            if (!p.paymentDate) return false;

            // Intentamos convertirlo a un objeto Date de JS
            const fechaPago = new Date(p.paymentDate);

            // Si es una fecha vÃ¡lida, comparamos aÃ±o, mes y dÃ­a exactos
            if (!isNaN(fechaPago.getTime())) {
              return (
                fechaPago.getDate() === hoy.getDate() &&
                fechaPago.getMonth() === hoy.getMonth() &&
                fechaPago.getFullYear() === hoy.getFullYear()
              );
            }
            const pStr = String(p.paymentDate);
            const anioStr = String(hoy.getFullYear());
            const mesStr = String(hoy.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, '0');
            const diaStr = String(hoy.getDate()).padStart(2, '0');

            return pStr.includes(anioStr) && pStr.includes(mesStr) && pStr.includes(diaStr);
          })
          .reduce((acc, p) => acc + (p.amount || 0), 0);

        this.todayRevenue.set(sumaHoy);
      },
      error: (err) => console.error('Error al cargar pagos del dÃ­a', err)
    });

    // 3. Cantidad de servicios pendientes
    this.attentionService.getAll().subscribe({
      next: (attentions) => {
        const pendientes = attentions.filter(a => !a.delivered);
        this.pendingAttentionsCount.set(pendientes.length);
      },
      error: (err) => console.error('Error al cargar atenciones', err)
    });
  }

}

import { Component, inject, OnInit, signal, OnDestroy } from '@angular/core';
import {
  ReactiveFormsModule,
  NonNullableFormBuilder,
  Validators
} from '@angular/forms';
import { ActivatedRoute, Router, RouterLink } from '@angular/router';

import { ItemService } from '../../../core/services/item.service';
import { ItemDtoRequest } from '../../../models/Item-model';

@Component({
  selector: 'app-item-edit',
  standalone: true,
  imports: [ReactiveFormsModule, RouterLink],
  templateUrl: './item-edit.component.html',
  styleUrl: './item-edit.component.css'
})
export class ItemEditComponent implements OnInit, OnDestroy {

  private fb = inject(NonNullableFormBuilder);
  private itemService = inject(ItemService);
  private route = inject(ActivatedRoute);
  private router = inject(Router);

  loading = signal(false);
  loadingImage = signal(false);
  saving = signal(false);
  errorMessage = signal('');
  
  selectedImage = signal<File | null>(null);
  existingImageUrl = signal<string | null>(null); // URL temporal de la foto actual
  itemId = signal<number | null>(null);
  itemLoaded = signal(false);
  selectedImagePreview = signal<string | null>(null);

  itemForm = this.fb.group({
    description: ['', [Validators.required]],
    quantity: [1, [Validators.required, Validators.min(1)]],
    unitPrice: [0, [Validators.required, Validators.min(0.01)]],
    isService: [false]
  });

  ngOnInit(): void {
    const idParam = this.route.snapshot.paramMap.get('id');
    const id = Number(idParam);

    if (idParam === null || !Number.isSafeInteger(id) || id <= 0) {
      this.errorMessage.set('El identificador del Ã­tem no es vÃ¡lido.');
      return;
    }

    this.itemId.set(id);
    this.loadItem(id);
  }

  loadItem(id: number): void {
    this.loading.set(true);
    this.errorMessage.set('');
    this.itemLoaded.set(false);

    this.itemService.getById(id).subscribe({
      next: (data) => {
        this.itemForm.patchValue({
          description: data.description,
          quantity: data.quantity,
          unitPrice: data.unitPrice,
          isService: data.isService ?? false
        });

        // Cargamos la imagen actual si el Ã­tem la posee
        this.loadExistingImage(id);

        this.loading.set(false);
        this.itemLoaded.set(true);
      },
      error: () => {
        this.errorMessage.set('No se pudieron cargar los datos del Ã­tem.');
        this.loading.set(false);
      }
    });
  }

  // Descarga la imagen en binario y crea una URL temporal de visualizaciÃ³n
  loadExistingImage(id: number): void {
    this.loadingImage.set(true);
    this.itemService.getItemImage(id).subscribe({
      next: (blob) => {
        if (blob && blob.size > 0) {
          const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
          this.existingImageUrl.set(url);
        }
        this.loadingImage.set(false);
      },
      error: () => {
        this.loadingImage.set(false);
      }
    });
  }

  // Elimina la imagen actual llamando al endpoint DELETE del backend
  removeExistingImage(): void {
    if (!window.confirm('Â¿EstÃ¡s seguro de eliminar la imagen actual?')) return;

    const id = this.itemId()!;
    this.itemService.deleteItemImage(id).subscribe({
      next: () => {
        const currentUrl = this.existingImageUrl();
        if (currentUrl) URL.revokeObjectURL(currentUrl);
        this.existingImageUrl.set(null);
      },
      error: () => {
        this.errorMessage.set('No se pudo eliminar la imagen.');
      }
    });
  }

  onImageSelected(event: Event): void {
    const input = event.target as HTMLInputElement;
    const file = input.files?.[0] ?? null;

    // Liberamos la URL anterior si existÃ­a para evitar fugas de memoria
    const currentPreview = this.selectedImagePreview();
    if (currentPreview) {
      URL.revokeObjectURL(currentPreview);
    }

    if (file) {
      this.selectedImage.set(file);
      // Generamos una URL temporal para previsualizar la foto seleccionada
      const previewUrl = URL.createObjectURL(file);
      this.selectedImagePreview.set(previewUrl);
    } else {
      this.selectedImage.set(null);
      this.selectedImagePreview.set(null);
    }
  }

  ngOnDestroy(): void {
    // Limpiamos ambas URLs al destruir el componente
    const url = this.existingImageUrl();
    if (url) URL.revokeObjectURL(url);

    const previewUrl = this.selectedImagePreview();
    if (previewUrl) URL.revokeObjectURL(previewUrl);
  }

  onSubmit(): void {
    if (!this.itemLoaded() || this.loading() || this.saving()) {
      return;
    }

    if (this.itemForm.invalid) {
      this.itemForm.markAllAsTouched();
      return;
    }

    this.saving.set(true);
    this.errorMessage.set('');

    const id = this.itemId()!;
    const request: ItemDtoRequest = this.itemForm.getRawValue();
    const image = this.selectedImage() ?? undefined;

    this.itemService.updateItem(id, request, image).subscribe({
      next: () => {
        this.saving.set(false);
        this.router.navigate(['/dashboard/item-list']);
      },
      error: () => {
        this.errorMessage.set('No se pudo actualizar el Ã­tem.');
        this.saving.set(false);
      }
    });
  }
}

  @if (errorMessage()) {
    <div class="alert alert-danger">
      <i class="bi bi-exclamation-triangle me-2"></i> {{ errorMessage() }}
    </div>
  }

  <!-- Formulario -->
  <form [formGroup]="itemForm" (ngSubmit)="onSubmit()" class="form-grid">

    <!-- DescripciÃ³n -->
    <div class="form-group">
      <label class="form-label" for="description">DescripciÃ³n *</label>
      <div class="input-wrapper">
        <span class="input-icon-prefix"><i class="bi bi-card-text"></i></span>
        <input
          id="description"
          type="text"
          class="form-control"
          formControlName="description"
          placeholder="Ej: Desayuno continental, Cerveza artesanal..."
        >
      </div>
      @if (itemForm.controls.description.touched && itemForm.controls.description.hasError('required')) {
        <small class="error-text">
          <i class="bi bi-info-circle me-1"></i> La descripciÃ³n es obligatoria.
        </small>
      }
    </div>

    <!-- Cantidad y Precio Unitario (En dos columnas) -->
    <div class="row-grid-inputs">
      <!-- Cantidad -->
      <div class="form-group">
        <label class="form-label" for="quantity">Cantidad *</label>
        <div class="input-wrapper">
          <span class="input-icon-prefix"><i class="bi bi-123"></i></span>
          <input
            id="quantity"
            type="number"
            min="1"
            step="1"
            class="form-control"
            formControlName="quantity"
          >
        </div>
        @if (itemForm.controls.quantity.touched) {
          @if (itemForm.controls.quantity.hasError('required')) {
            <small class="error-text">La cantidad es obligatoria.</small>
          }
          @if (itemForm.controls.quantity.hasError('min')) {
            <small class="error-text">MÃ­nimo 1.</small>
          }
        }
      </div>

      <!-- Precio unitario -->
      <div class="form-group">
        <label class="form-label" for="unitPrice">Precio unitario ($) *</label>
        <div class="input-wrapper">
          <span class="input-icon-prefix"><i class="bi bi-currency-dollar"></i></span>
          <input
            id="unitPrice"
            type="number"
            min="0.01"
            step="0.01"
            class="form-control"
            formControlName="unitPrice"
            placeholder="0.00"
          >
        </div>
        @if (itemForm.controls.unitPrice.touched) {
          @if (itemForm.controls.unitPrice.hasError('required')) {
            <small class="error-text">El precio es obligatorio.</small>
          }
          @if (itemForm.controls.unitPrice.hasError('min')) {
            <small class="error-text">Debe ser mayor a cero.</small>
          }
        }
      </div>
    </div>

    <!-- Tipo de Ã­tem (Checkbox estilizado) -->
    <div class="form-group checkbox-group">
      <label class="checkbox-label" for="isService">
        <input
          id="isService"
          type="checkbox"
          formControlName="isService"
        >
        <span class="checkbox-custom"></span>
        <span>Este Ã­tem corresponde a un servicio</span>
      </label>
    </div>

    <!-- Imagen opcional -->
    <div class="form-group">
      <label class="form-label" for="image">Imagen (opcional)</label>
      <div class="input-wrapper file-input-wrapper">
        <input
          id="image"
          type="file"
          accept="image/*"
          class="form-control-file"
          (change)="onImageSelected($event)"
        >
      </div>
      @if (selectedImage()) {
        <small class="subtitle mt-1 d-block">
          <i class="bi bi-file-earmark-image me-1"></i> Archivo seleccionado: <strong>{{ selectedImage()?.name }}</strong>
        </small>
      }
    </div>

    <!-- Acciones -->
    <div class="form-actions">
      <button
        type="submit"
        class="btn-primary-action"
        [disabled]="saving()"
      >
        @if (saving()) {
          <i class="bi bi-arrow-repeat spin me-2"></i> Guardando...
        } @else {
          <i class="bi bi-check-lg me-2"></i> Guardar Ã­tem
        }
      </button>

      <a routerLink="/items" class="btn-secondary-action">
        Cancelar
      </a>
    </div>
  </form>
</div>

import { Component, inject, signal } from '@angular/core';
import {
  ReactiveFormsModule,
  NonNullableFormBuilder,
  Validators
} from '@angular/forms';
import { Router, RouterLink } from '@angular/router';

import { ItemService } from '../../../core/services/item.service';
import { ItemDtoRequest } from '../../../models/Item-model';

@Component({
  selector: 'app-item-form',
  standalone: true,
  imports: [ReactiveFormsModule, RouterLink],
  templateUrl: './item-form.component.html',
  styleUrl: './item-form.component.css'
})
export class ItemFormComponent {

  private fb = inject(NonNullableFormBuilder);
  private itemService = inject(ItemService);
  private router = inject(Router);

  saving = signal(false);
  errorMessage = signal('');
  selectedImage = signal<File | null>(null);

  itemForm = this.fb.group({
    description: ['', [
      Validators.required
    ]],
    quantity: [1, [
      Validators.required,
      Validators.min(1)
    ]],
    unitPrice: [0, [
      Validators.required,
      Validators.min(0.01)
    ]],
    isService: [false]
  });

  onImageSelected(event: Event): void {
    const input = event.target as HTMLInputElement;
    const file = input.files?.[0] ?? null;

    this.selectedImage.set(file);
  }

  onSubmit(): void {
    if (this.itemForm.invalid) {
      this.itemForm.markAllAsTouched();
      return;
    }

    if (this.saving()) return;

    this.saving.set(true);
    this.errorMessage.set('');

    const request: ItemDtoRequest = this.itemForm.getRawValue();

    const image = this.selectedImage() ?? undefined;

    this.itemService.createItem(request, image).subscribe({
      next: () => {
        this.saving.set(false);
        this.router.navigate(['dashboard/item-list']);
      },
      error: () => {
        this.errorMessage.set('No se pudo crear el Ã­tem.');
        this.saving.set(false);
      }
    });
  }
}

import { Component, inject, OnInit, OnDestroy, signal } from '@angular/core';
import { RouterLink } from '@angular/router';

import { ItemService } from '../../../core/services/item.service';
import { ItemDtoResponse } from '../../../models/Item-model';
import { CurrencyPipe } from '@angular/common';

interface ItemWithImage extends ItemDtoResponse {
  imageUrl?: string;
}

@Component({
  selector: 'app-item-list',
  standalone: true,
  imports: [RouterLink, CurrencyPipe],
  templateUrl: './item-list.component.html',
  styleUrl: './item-list.component.css'
})
export class ItemListComponent implements OnInit, OnDestroy {

  private itemService = inject(ItemService);

  items = signal<ItemWithImage[]>([]);

  loading = signal(false);
  errorMessage = signal('');
  deletingId = signal<number | null>(null);

  ngOnInit(): void {
    this.loadItems();
  }

  loadItems(): void {
    this.loading.set(true);
    this.errorMessage.set('');

    this.clearImages();
    this.items.set([]);

    this.itemService.getAll().subscribe({
      next: (data) => {
        this.items.set(data);
        this.loading.set(false);

        data.forEach(item => {
          this.loadItemImage(item.id);
        });
      },
      error: () => {
        this.errorMessage.set('No se pudieron cargar los Ã­tems.');
        this.loading.set(false);
      }
    });
  }

  //Obtiene la imagen de cada Ã­tem y genera una URL temporal para mostrarla
  loadItemImage(id: number): void {
    this.itemService.getItemImage(id).subscribe({
      next: (blob) => {
        if (blob.size === 0) return;

        const imageUrl = URL.createObjectURL(blob);

        const itemExists = this.items().some(item => item.id === id);

        if (!itemExists) {
          URL.revokeObjectURL(imageUrl);
          return;
        }

        this.items.update(items =>
          items.map(item =>
            item.id === id
              ? { ...item, imageUrl }
              : item
          )
        );
      },
      error: () => {
        // Un Ã­tem puede no tener imagen.
      }
    });
  }

  //elimina un registro y actualiza el listado solamente cuando el backend confirma la operaciÃ³n
  deleteItem(id: number): void {
    if (this.deletingId() !== null) return;

    this.deletingId.set(id);
    this.errorMessage.set('');

    this.itemService.deleteItem(id).subscribe({
      next: () => {
        const deletedItem = this.items().find(item => item.id === id);

        if (deletedItem?.imageUrl) {
          URL.revokeObjectURL(deletedItem.imageUrl);
        }

        this.items.update(items =>
          items.filter(item => item.id !== id)
        );

        this.deletingId.set(null);
      },
      error: () => {
        this.errorMessage.set('No se pudo eliminar el Ã­tem.');
        this.deletingId.set(null);
      }
    });
  }

  private clearImages(): void {
    this.items().forEach(item => {
      if (item.imageUrl) {
        URL.revokeObjectURL(item.imageUrl);
      }
    });
  }

  ngOnDestroy(): void {
    this.clearImages();
  }
}

              </td>

              <!-- Columna Fecha -->
              <td class="subtitle">{{ payment.paymentDate | date:'short' }}</td>

              <!-- Columna MÃ©todo de Pago -->
              <td>
                <span class="badge badge-secondary">
                  <i class="bi bi-credit-card me-1"></i> {{ payment.paymentMethod || 'N/D' }}
                </span>
              </td>

              <!-- Columna Referencia -->
              <td class="subtitle" style="padding-right: 1.5rem;">
                {{ payment.transactionReference || 'Sin referencia' }}
              </td>
            </tr>
          }
        </tbody>
      </table>
    </div>
  }

</div>
import { Component, OnInit, ChangeDetectionStrategy, signal } from '@angular/core';
import { CommonModule } from '@angular/common';
import { PaymentService } from '../../core/services/payment.service';
import { AccountService } from '../../core/services/account.service';
import { PaymentDTOResponse } from '../../models/Payment-model';

// Interfaz extendida para incluir los datos del huÃ©sped de forma limpia
export interface PaymentWithGuest extends PaymentDTOResponse {
  guestName?: string;
  guestSurname?: string;
}

@Component({
  selector: 'app-payment-list',
  standalone: true,
  imports: [CommonModule],
  templateUrl: './payment-list.component.html',
  styleUrls: ['./payment-list.component.css'],
  changeDetection: ChangeDetectionStrategy.OnPush
})
export class PaymentListComponent implements OnInit {
  // --- Estados con Signals ---
  payments = signal<PaymentWithGuest[]>([]);
  loading = signal<boolean>(true);
  errorMessage = signal<string>('');

  constructor(
    private paymentService: PaymentService,
    private accountService: AccountService
  ) {}

  ngOnInit(): void {
    this.loadPayments();
  }

  loadPayments(): void {
    this.loading.set(true);
    this.errorMessage.set('');
    
    this.paymentService.getAllPayments().subscribe({
      next: (data: PaymentDTOResponse[]) => {
        const rawPayments: PaymentWithGuest[] = Array.isArray(data) ? [...data] : [];
        this.payments.set(rawPayments);
        this.loading.set(false);

        // Recorremos cada pago para buscar los datos del titular usando el accountId
        rawPayments.forEach(payment => {
          if (payment.accountId) {
            this.accountService.getAccountById(payment.accountId).subscribe({
              next: (account: any) => {
                if (account) {
                  payment.guestName = account.user?.name || account.name || '';
                  payment.guestSurname = account.user?.surname || account.surname || '';
                  // Actualizamos la seÃ±al con el array modificado para que OnPush detecte el cambio reactivamente
                  this.payments.set([...rawPayments]);
                }
              },
              error: (err) => console.error(`Error al cargar cuenta #${payment.accountId}`, err)
            });
          }
        });
      },
      error: (err: any) => {
        this.errorMessage.set('No se pudieron cargar los pagos o no cuentas con los permisos necesarios.');
        this.loading.set(false);
        console.error(err);
      }
    });
  }
}

            <p class="subtitle">Esta habitaciÃ³n no tiene imÃ¡genes cargadas.</p>
          } @else {
            <div class="edit-images-grid">

import { Component, inject, OnInit, signal, OnDestroy } from '@angular/core';
import {
  ReactiveFormsModule,
  NonNullableFormBuilder,
  Validators
} from '@angular/forms';
import { ActivatedRoute, Router, RouterLink } from '@angular/router';

import { RoomService } from '../../../core/services/room.service';
import { RoomTypeService } from '../../../core/services/room-type.service';
import { RoomTypeDTOResponse } from '../../../models/RoomType-model';
import { RoomDtoResponse } from '../../../models/Room-model';

@Component({
  selector: 'app-room-edit',
  standalone: true,
  imports: [ReactiveFormsModule, RouterLink],
  templateUrl: './room-edit.component.html',
  styleUrl: './room-edit.component.css'
})
export class RoomEditComponent implements OnInit, OnDestroy {

  private fb = inject(NonNullableFormBuilder);
  private roomService = inject(RoomService);
  private roomTypeService = inject(RoomTypeService);
  private route = inject(ActivatedRoute);
  private router = inject(Router);

  roomId = 0;

  roomTypes = signal<RoomTypeDTOResponse[]>([]);
  selectedImages = signal<File[]>([]); // Nuevas imÃ¡genes a subir
  selectedImagePreviews = signal<string[]>([]); // Vistas previas de las nuevas imÃ¡genes
  existingImages = signal<string[]>([]); // URLs temporales de las imÃ¡genes actuales

  loading = signal(true);
  loadingImages = signal(false);
  saving = signal(false);
  errorMessage = signal('');

  roomForm = this.fb.group({
    number: [0, [Validators.required, Validators.min(1)]],
    roomTypeId: [0, [Validators.required, Validators.min(1)]]
  });

  ngOnInit(): void {
    const id = Number(this.route.snapshot.paramMap.get('id'));

    if (!Number.isInteger(id) || id <= 0) {
      this.errorMessage.set('El ID de la habitaciÃ³n no es vÃ¡lido.');
      this.loading.set(false);
      return;
    }

    this.roomId = id;
    this.loadRoomData();
  }

  loadRoomData(): void {
    this.loading.set(true);
    this.errorMessage.set('');

    this.roomService.getById(this.roomId).subscribe({
      next: (room: RoomDtoResponse) => {
        this.roomForm.patchValue({
          number: Number(room.number),
          roomTypeId: room.roomTypeDTOResponse?.id ?? 0
        });

        this.loadRoomTypes();
        this.loadExistingImages(room);
      },
      error: () => {
        this.errorMessage.set('No se pudo cargar la habitaciÃ³n.');
        this.loading.set(false);
      }
    });
  }

  loadRoomTypes(): void {
    this.roomTypeService.getAll().subscribe({
      next: (data) => {
        this.roomTypes.set(data);
        this.loading.set(false);
      },
      error: () => {
        this.errorMessage.set('No se pudieron cargar los tipos de habitaciÃ³n.');
        this.loading.set(false);
      }
    });
  }

  loadExistingImages(room: RoomDtoResponse): void {
    const total = room.imagesCount ?? 0;
    if (total === 0) return;

    this.loadingImages.set(true);
    const urls: string[] = [];
    let descargadas = 0;

    for (let i = 0; i < total; i++) {
      this.roomService.getRoomImage(room.id, i).subscribe({
        next: (blob) => {
          urls[i] = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
          descargadas++;
          if (descargadas === total) {
            this.existingImages.set(urls);
            this.loadingImages.set(false);
          }
        },
        error: () => {
          descargadas++;
          if (descargadas === total) {
            this.loadingImages.set(false);
          }
        }
      });
    }
  }

  removeExistingImage(index: number): void {
    if (!window.confirm('Â¿EstÃ¡s seguro de eliminar esta imagen?')) return;

    this.roomService.deleteRoomImage(this.roomId, index).subscribe({
      next: (roomActualizada) => {
        URL.revokeObjectURL(this.existingImages()[index]);
        this.existingImages.set([]);
        this.loadExistingImages(roomActualizada);
      },
      error: () => {
        this.errorMessage.set('No se pudo eliminar la imagen.');
      }
    });
  }

  // Captura los archivos nuevos y genera sus miniaturas temporales
  onImagesSelected(event: Event): void {
    const input = event.target as HTMLInputElement;
    if (input.files) {
      // Limpiamos previsualizaciones anteriores para evitar fugas de memoria
      this.selectedImagePreviews().forEach(url => URL.revokeObjectURL(url));

      const files = Array.from(input.files);
      this.selectedImages.set(files);

      const previews = files.map(file => URL.createObjectURL(file));
      this.selectedImagePreviews.set(previews);
    }
  }

  updateRoom(): void {
    if (this.roomForm.invalid || this.saving() || this.loading()) {
      this.roomForm.markAllAsTouched();
      return;
    }

    this.saving.set(true);
    this.errorMessage.set('');

    const request = this.roomForm.getRawValue();

    this.roomService.updateRoom(this.roomId, request).subscribe({
      next: () => {
        const newImages = this.selectedImages();

        if (newImages.length > 0) {
          this.roomService.addImages(this.roomId, newImages).subscribe({
            next: () => {
              this.saving.set(false);
              this.router.navigate(['/dashboard/room-list']);
            },
            error: () => {
              this.errorMessage.set('Datos actualizados, pero fallÃ³ la subida de nuevas imÃ¡genes.');
              this.saving.set(false);
            }
          });
        } else {
          this.saving.set(false);
          this.router.navigate(['/dashboard/room-list']);
        }
      },
      error: () => {
        this.errorMessage.set('No se pudo actualizar la habitaciÃ³n.');
        this.saving.set(false);
      }
    });
  }

  ngOnDestroy(): void {
    // Limpieza de todas las URLs en memoria
    this.existingImages().forEach(url => URL.revokeObjectURL(url));
    this.selectedImagePreviews().forEach(url => URL.revokeObjectURL(url));
  }
}

}

  <!-- TÃ­tulo Principal -->
  <h1>
    <i class="bi bi-plus-square"></i> Crear nueva habitaciÃ³n
  </h1>

        </p>

        </p>

          id="roomTypeId"

        >
          <option [ngValue]="0" disabled>
            Seleccione un tipo de habitaciÃ³n

              {{ type.name }} (${{ type.pricePerNight }} - Cap. {{ type.capacity }})

        <p class="error-message">
          <i class="bi bi-info-circle"></i> Debe seleccionar un tipo de habitaciÃ³n.
        </p>
      }
    </div>

      >

      @if (selectedImages().length > 0) {

import { Component, inject, OnInit, signal, OnDestroy } from '@angular/core';
import {
  ReactiveFormsModule,
  NonNullableFormBuilder,
  Validators
} from '@angular/forms';
import { Router, RouterLink, ActivatedRoute } from '@angular/router';

import { RoomService } from '../../../core/services/room.service';
import { RoomTypeService } from '../../../core/services/room-type.service';
import { RoomTypeDTOResponse } from '../../../models/RoomType-model';

@Component({
  selector: 'app-room-form',
  standalone: true,
  imports: [ReactiveFormsModule, RouterLink],
  templateUrl: './room-form.component.html',
  styleUrl: './room-form.component.css'
})
export class RoomFormComponent implements OnInit, OnDestroy {

  private fb = inject(NonNullableFormBuilder);
  private roomService = inject(RoomService);
  private roomTypeService = inject(RoomTypeService);
  private router = inject(Router);
  private route = inject(ActivatedRoute);

  roomTypes = signal<RoomTypeDTOResponse[]>([]);
  selectedImages = signal<File[]>([]);
  imagePreviews = signal<string[]>([]); // SeÃ±al para almacenar las URLs temporales de las miniaturas

  loadingTypes = signal(false);
  saving = signal(false);
  errorMessage = signal('');

  roomForm = this.fb.group({
    number: [0, [Validators.required, Validators.min(1)]],
    roomTypeId: [0, [Validators.required, Validators.min(1)]],
    state: ['AVAILABLE', Validators.required]
  });

  ngOnInit(): void {
    this.loadRoomTypes();
    this.checkUrlParameters(); 
  }

  loadRoomTypes(): void {
    this.loadingTypes.set(true);
    this.errorMessage.set('');

    this.roomTypeService.getAll().subscribe({
      next: (data) => {
        this.roomTypes.set(data);
        this.loadingTypes.set(false);
      },
      error: () => {
        this.errorMessage.set('No se pudieron cargar los tipos de habitaciÃ³n.');
        this.loadingTypes.set(false);
      }
    });
  }

  checkUrlParameters(): void {
    const typeIdParam = this.route.snapshot.queryParamMap.get('roomTypeId');
    
    if (typeIdParam) {
      const parsedId = Number(typeIdParam);
      this.roomForm.patchValue({ roomTypeId: parsedId });
    }
  }

  // Captura los archivos y genera las URLs para las miniaturas
  onImagesSelected(event: Event): void {
    const input = event.target as HTMLInputElement;
    if (input.files) {
      // Limpiamos URLs anteriores para evitar fugas de memoria si selecciona varias veces
      this.imagePreviews().forEach(url => URL.revokeObjectURL(url));

      const files = Array.from(input.files);
      this.selectedImages.set(files);

      // Generamos las vistas previas en miniatura
      const previews = files.map(file => URL.createObjectURL(file));
      this.imagePreviews.set(previews);
    }
  }

  createRoom(): void {
    if (this.roomForm.invalid || this.saving()) {
      this.roomForm.markAllAsTouched();
      return;
    }

    this.saving.set(true);
    this.errorMessage.set('');

    const request = this.roomForm.getRawValue();

    this.roomService.createRoom(request, this.selectedImages()).subscribe({
      next: () => {
        this.saving.set(false);
        this.router.navigate(['/dashboard/room-list']);
      },
      error: () => {
        this.errorMessage.set('No se pudo crear la habitaciÃ³n.');
        this.saving.set(false);
      }
    });
  }

  ngOnDestroy(): void {
    // Liberamos las URLs temporales de memoria al destruir el componente
    this.imagePreviews().forEach(url => URL.revokeObjectURL(url));
  }
}
