<section class="room-list-container">

  <!-- SecciÃ³n de Encabezado -->
  <div class="header-section">
    <h2><i class="fa-solid fa-door-closed"></i> GestiÃ³n de Habitaciones</h2>
    <a routerLink="/dashboard/room-type" class="btn-primary">
      <i class="fa-solid fa-plus"></i> Crear Tipo de habitaciÃ³n
    </a>
  </div>

  <hr class="divider" />

  <!-- Contenedor de Tarjetas de Tipos de HabitaciÃ³n -->
  <div class="room-types-grid">
    @for (type of roomTypes(); track type.id) {
      <div class="room-type-card">
        
        <!-- InformaciÃ³n del Tipo de HabitaciÃ³n -->
        <div class="room-type-info">
          <h3><i class="fa-solid fa-tag"></i> {{ type.name }}</h3>
          <p class="room-type-desc">{{ type.description || 'Sin descripciÃ³n disponible.' }}</p>
          
          <div class="room-type-details">
            <span><i class="fa-solid fa-user-group"></i> Capacidad: <strong>{{ type.capacity }} pax</strong></span>
            <span><i class="fa-solid fa-dollar-sign"></i> Precio: <strong>${{ type.pricePerNight }}</strong></span>
          </div>
        </div>

        <!-- Botones de AcciÃ³n (Crear, Editar, Eliminar) -->
        <div class="room-type-actions">
          <button 
            type="button" 
            class="btn-info-action" 
            title="Crear Habitacion de este tipo"
            (click)="nuevaHabitacion(type.id)">
            <i class="fa-solid fa-plus"></i>
            Nueva Habitacion
          </button>

          <a
            [routerLink]="['/dashboard/room-type-edit', type.id]"
            class="btn-warning"
            title="Editar tipo de habitaciÃ³n"
          >
            <i class="fa-solid fa-pen-to-square"></i>
          </a>

          <button 
            type="button" 
            class="btn-danger" 
            title="Eliminar tipo de habitaciÃ³n"
            (click)="deleteRoomType(type.id)">
            <i class="fa-solid fa-trash-can"></i>
          </button>
        </div>

      </div>
    } @empty {
      <div class="alert alert-warning room-types-empty">
        <i class="fa-solid fa-circle-info"></i> No hay tipos de habitaciÃ³n registrados.
      </div>
    }
  </div>

  <!-- Mensajes de Error de AcciÃ³n -->
  @if (actionError()) {
    <div class="alert alert-danger">
      <i class="fa-solid fa-triangle-exclamation"></i> {{ actionError() }}
    </div>
  }

  <!-- Mensajes de Carga, Error general o Listado VacÃ­o -->
  @if (loading()) {
    <div class="alert alert-info">
      <i class="fa-solid fa-spinner fa-spin"></i> Cargando habitaciones...
    </div>
  } @else if (errorMessage()) {
    <div class="alert alert-danger error-alert-content">
      <div class="error-message-wrapper">
        <span><i class="fa-solid fa-triangle-exclamation"></i> {{ errorMessage() }}</span>
        <button type="button" class="btn-warning" (click)="loadRooms()">
          <i class="fa-solid fa-rotate-right"></i> Reintentar
        </button>
      </div>
    </div>
  } @else if (rooms().length === 0) {
    <div class="alert alert-warning">
      <i class="fa-solid fa-circle-info"></i> No hay habitaciones registradas.
    </div>
  } @else {

    <!-- Tabla de Datos -->
    <div class="table-wrapper">
      <table class="table">
        <thead>
          <tr>
            <th>NÃºmero</th>
            <th>Tipo</th>
            <th>Capacidad</th>
            <th>Estado</th>
            <th>Precio por noche</th>
            <th class="text-right">Acciones</th>
          </tr>
        </thead>
        <tbody>
          @for (room of rooms(); track room.id) {
            <tr>
              <td><strong>#{{ room.number }}</strong></td>

              <td>
                {{ room.roomTypeDTOResponse?.name 
                    ?? room.roomTypeName 
                    ?? 'Sin tipo' }}
              </td>

              <td>
                <i class="fa-solid fa-user-group"></i> 
                {{ room.roomTypeDTOResponse?.capacity 
                    ?? room.capacity 
                    ?? 'Sin datos' }}
              </td>

              <td>
                <span
                  class="badge"
                  [class.badge-success]="room.state === 'AVAILABLE'"
                  [class.badge-danger]="room.state === 'OCCUPIED'"
                  [class.badge-warning]="room.state === 'MAINTENANCE'"
                  [class.badge-secondary]="room.state === 'CLEANING'"
                >
                  @switch (room.state) {
                    @case ('AVAILABLE') {
                      Disponible
                    }
                    @case ('OCCUPIED') {
                      Ocupada
                    }
                    @case ('MAINTENANCE') {
                      Mantenimiento
                    }
                    @case ('CLEANING') {
                      Limpieza
                    }
                    @default {
                      {{ room.state }}
                    }
                  }
                </span>
              </td>

              <td>
                <strong>${{ room.roomTypeDTOResponse?.pricePerNight 
                    ?? room.pricePerNight 
                    ?? 'Sin datos' }}</strong>
              </td>

              <td>
                <div class="table-actions">
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    class="btn-info-action"
                    (click)="openRoomModal(room)"
                    title="Ver detalle"
                  >
                    <i class="fa-solid fa-eye"></i>
                  </button>

                  <a
                    [routerLink]="['/dashboard/room-edit', room.id]"
                    class="btn-warning"
                    title="Editar"
                  >
                    <i class="fa-solid fa-pen-to-square"></i>
                  </a>

                  <button
                    type="button"
                    class="btn-secondary-action"
                    (click)="putInMaintenance(room.id)"
                    [title]="room.state === 'MAINTENANCE' ? 'Quitar de mantenimiento' : 'Poner en mantenimiento'"
                  >
                    <i class="fa-solid fa-screwdriver-wrench"></i>
                  </button>

                  <button
                    type="button"
                    class="btn-danger"
                    (click)="deleteRoom(room.id)"
                    title="Eliminar"
                  >
                    <i class="fa-solid fa-trash-can"></i>
                  </button>
                </div>
              </td>

            </tr>
          }
        </tbody>
      </table>
    </div>
  }

</section>


<!-- MODAL DE DETALLE -->
@if (selectedRoom(); as room) {
  <div class="modal-backdrop" (click)="closeRoomModal()">
    <div
      class="modal-card"
      role="dialog"
      aria-modal="true"
      aria-labelledby="room-modal-title"
      (click)="$event.stopPropagation()"
    >

      <!-- BotÃ³n Cerrar -->
      <button
        type="button"
        class="btn-close"
        aria-label="Cerrar modal"
        (click)="closeRoomModal()"
      >
        <i class="fa-solid fa-xmark"></i>
      </button>

      <!-- Encabezado del Modal -->
      <div class="modal-header-profile">
        <h3 id="room-modal-title">
          HabitaciÃ³n #{{ room.number }}
        </h3>
        <p class="subtitle">
          {{ room.roomTypeDTOResponse?.name ?? room.roomTypeName ?? 'EstÃ¡ndar' }}
        </p>
      </div>

      <!-- CuadrÃ­cula de Detalles -->
      <div class="detail-grid">
        <div class="detail-item">
          <span class="detail-label"><i class="fa-solid fa-circle-check"></i> Estado:</span>
          <span class="detail-value">{{ room.state }}</span>
        </div>
        <div class="detail-item">
          <span class="detail-label"><i class="fa-solid fa-user-group"></i> Capacidad:</span>
          <span class="detail-value">
            {{ room.roomTypeDTOResponse?.capacity ?? room.capacity ?? 'Sin datos' }} personas
          </span>
        </div>
        <div class="detail-item detail-item-full">
          <span class="detail-label"><i class="fa-solid fa-dollar-sign"></i> Precio por noche:</span>
          <span class="detail-value">
            ${{ room.roomTypeDTOResponse?.pricePerNight ?? room.pricePerNight ?? 'Sin datos' }}
          </span>
        </div>
      </div>

      <!-- SecciÃ³n de ImÃ¡genes -->
      <div class="modal-gallery-section">
        <h3><i class="fa-solid fa-images"></i> ImÃ¡genes de la habitaciÃ³n</h3>

        @if (loadingImages()) {
          <div class="alert alert-info modal-alert-margin">
            <i class="fa-solid fa-spinner fa-spin"></i> Cargando imÃ¡genes...
          </div>
        } @else if (imageError()) {
          <div class="alert alert-danger modal-alert-margin">
            <i class="fa-solid fa-triangle-exclamation"></i> {{ imageError() }}
          </div>
        } @else if (roomImages().length === 0) {
          <p class="subtitle modal-subtitle-margin">Esta habitaciÃ³n no tiene imÃ¡genes.</p>
        } @else {
          <div class="image-gallery">
            <img
              [src]="roomImages()[currentImageIndex()]"
              [alt]="'Imagen de la habitaciÃ³n ' + room.number"
              class="room-image-preview"
            >

            @if (roomImages().length > 1) {
              <div class="gallery-controls">
                <button
                  type="button"
                  class="btn-secondary-action"
                  (click)="previousImage()"
                  aria-label="Imagen anterior"
                >
                  <i class="fa-solid fa-chevron-left"></i> Anterior
                </button>

                <span>
                  {{ currentImageIndex() + 1 }} de {{ roomImages().length }}
                </span>

                <button
                  type="button"
                  class="btn-secondary-action"
                  (click)="nextImage()"
                  aria-label="Imagen siguiente"
                >
                  Siguiente <i class="fa-solid fa-chevron-right"></i>
                </button>
              </div>
            }
          </div>
        }
      </div>

      <!-- Acciones del Modal -->
      <div class="modal-actions">
        <button
          type="button"
          class="btn-primary"
          (click)="closeRoomModal()"
        >
          Cerrar
        </button>
      </div>

    </div>
  </div>
}
import { Component, inject, OnInit, OnDestroy, signal } from '@angular/core';
import { Router, RouterLink } from '@angular/router';
import { Subscription } from 'rxjs';

import { RoomService } from '../../../core/services/room.service';
import { RoomTypeService } from '../../../core/services/room-type.service';
import { RoomDtoResponse } from '../../../models/Room-model';
import { RoomTypeDTOResponse } from '../../../models/RoomType-model';

@Component({
  selector: 'app-room-list',
  standalone: true,
  imports: [RouterLink],
  templateUrl: './room-list.component.html',
  styleUrl: './room-list.component.css'
})
export class RoomListComponent implements OnInit, OnDestroy {

  private roomService = inject(RoomService);
  private roomTypeService = inject(RoomTypeService);
  private route = inject(Router);

  rooms = signal<RoomDtoResponse[]>([]);
  roomTypes = signal<RoomTypeDTOResponse[]>([]);
  loading = signal(true);
  errorMessage = signal('');
  actionError = signal('');

  selectedRoom = signal<RoomDtoResponse | null>(null);

  // SeÃ±ales para el manejo de imÃ¡genes
  roomImages = signal<string[]>([]);
  loadingImages = signal(false);
  imageError = signal('');
  currentImageIndex = signal(0);

  private imageSubscription?: Subscription;

  ngOnInit(): void {
    this.loadRooms();
    this.loadRoomTypes();
  }

  loadRooms(): void {
    this.loading.set(true);
    this.errorMessage.set('');

    this.roomService.getAll().subscribe({
      next: (data) => {
        this.rooms.set(Array.isArray(data) ? data : []);
        this.loading.set(false);
      },
      error: () => {
        this.errorMessage.set('No se pudieron cargar las habitaciones.');
        this.loading.set(false);
      }
    });
  }

  loadRoomTypes(): void {
    this.roomTypeService.getAll().subscribe({
      next: (data) => {
        this.roomTypes.set(Array.isArray(data) ? data : []);
      },
      error: () => {
        console.error('No se pudieron cargar los tipos de habitaciÃ³n.');
      }
    });
  }

  deleteRoom(id: number): void {
    this.actionError.set('');

    this.roomService.deleteRoom(id).subscribe({
      next: () => {
        // Creamos un nuevo arreglo excluyendo la habitaciÃ³n eliminada
        const habitacionesActualizadas = this.rooms().filter(room => room.id !== id);
        this.rooms.set(habitacionesActualizadas);
      },
      error: () => {
        this.actionError.set('No se pudo eliminar la habitaciÃ³n.');
      }
    });
  }

 putInMaintenance(id: number): void {
    this.actionError.set('');

    this.roomService.putInMaintenance(id).subscribe({
      next: (roomActualizada) => {
        // Obtenemos el nuevo estado que determinÃ³ el backend ('MAINTENANCE' o 'AVAILABLE')
        const nuevoEstado = roomActualizada.state;

        // Recorremos la lista y actualizamos el estado de la habitaciÃ³n correspondiente
        const listaModificada = this.rooms().map(room => {
          if (room.id === id) {
            return { ...room, state: nuevoEstado };
          }
          return room;
        });

        this.rooms.set(listaModificada);

        // Si la habitaciÃ³n seleccionada en el modal es la misma, actualizamos su estado tambiÃ©n
        if (this.selectedRoom()?.id === id) {
          const habSeleccionada = this.selectedRoom();
          if (habSeleccionada) {
            this.selectedRoom.set({ ...habSeleccionada, state: nuevoEstado });
          }
        }
      },
      error: () => {
        this.actionError.set('No se pudo cambiar el estado de la habitaciÃ³n.');
      }
    });
  }

  openRoomModal(room: RoomDtoResponse): void {
    this.closeRoomModal(); 
    this.selectedRoom.set(room); 
    this.loadRoomImagesSimple(room);
  }

  
  loadRoomImagesSimple(room: RoomDtoResponse): void {
    const totalImagenes = room.imagesCount ?? 0;

    this.loadingImages.set(totalImagenes > 0);
    this.imageError.set('');
    this.currentImageIndex.set(0);

    if (totalImagenes === 0) {
      return;
    }

    const urlsTemporales: string[] = [];
    let imagenesDescargadas = 0;

    // Recorremos con un bucle
    for (let i = 0; i < totalImagenes; i++) {
      this.roomService.getRoomImage(room.id, i).subscribe({
        next: (blob) => {
          // Validamos que el usuario siga viendo la misma habitaciÃ³n
          if (this.selectedRoom()?.id !== room.id) return;

          // Convertimos el archivo binario a URL web
          const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
          urlsTemporales[i] = url; // Guardamos en la posiciÃ³n exacta
          imagenesDescargadas++;

          // Cuando terminamos de descargar todas las imÃ¡genes, actualizamos la seÃ±al
          if (imagenesDescargadas === totalImagenes) {
            this.roomImages.set(urlsTemporales);
            this.loadingImages.set(false);
          }
        },
        error: () => {
          if (this.selectedRoom()?.id === room.id) {
            this.imageError.set('No se pudieron cargar las imÃ¡genes.');
            this.loadingImages.set(false);
          }
        }
      });
    }
  }

  previousImage(): void {
    const total = this.roomImages().length;
    if (total === 0) return;

    let nuevoIndice = this.currentImageIndex() - 1;
    if (nuevoIndice < 0) {
      nuevoIndice = total - 1; // Si llega al principio, vuelve a la Ãºltima foto
    }
    this.currentImageIndex.set(nuevoIndice);
  }

  nextImage(): void {
    const total = this.roomImages().length;
    if (total === 0) return;

    let nuevoIndice = this.currentImageIndex() + 1;
    if (nuevoIndice >= total) {
      nuevoIndice = 0; // Si llega al final, vuelve a la primera foto
    }
    this.currentImageIndex.set(nuevoIndice);
  }

  closeRoomModal(): void {
    this.imageSubscription?.unsubscribe();
    this.imageSubscription = undefined;

    // Liberamos memoria de las URLs creadas
    this.roomImages().forEach(url => URL.revokeObjectURL(url));

    this.roomImages.set([]);
    this.selectedRoom.set(null);
    this.loadingImages.set(false);
    this.imageError.set('');
    this.currentImageIndex.set(0);
  }

  ngOnDestroy(): void {
    this.closeRoomModal();
  }

  nuevaHabitacion(roomTypeId?: number): void {
    if (roomTypeId) {
      this.route.navigate(['/dashboard/room-form'], { 
        queryParams: { roomTypeId } 
      });
    } else {
      this.route.navigate(['/dashboard/room-form']);
    }
  }

  deleteRoomType(id: number): void {
    if (!window.confirm('Â¿EstÃ¡s seguro de que deseas eliminar este tipo de habitaciÃ³n?')) {
      return;
    }
    this.actionError.set('');

    this.roomTypeService.deleteRoomType(id).subscribe({
      next: () => {
        const tiposActualizados = this.roomTypes().filter(type => type.id !== id);
        this.roomTypes.set(tiposActualizados);
      },
      error: (err) => {
        console.error('Error al eliminar tipo de habitaciÃ³n:', err);
        const msg = err.error?.message || 'No se pudo eliminar el tipo de habitaciÃ³n. Es posible que existan habitaciones asociadas.';
        this.actionError.set(msg);
      }
    });
  }
}

<section class="edit-container">
  <header class="edit-header">
    <h2>Editar Tipo de HabitaciÃ³n</h2>
    <a routerLink="/room-types" class="btn-back">Volver al listado</a>
  </header>

  @if (isLoading()) {
    <div class="state-message">Cargando datos del tipo de habitaciÃ³n...</div>
  } @else {
    @if (errorMessage()) {
      <div class="alert-error">{{ errorMessage() }}</div>
    }

    <form [formGroup]="editForm" (ngSubmit)="onSubmit()" class="edit-form">
      <!-- Nombre -->
      <div class="form-group">
        <label for="name">Nombre *</label>
        <input
          id="name"
          type="text"
          formControlName="name"
          placeholder="Ej: Suite Ejecutiva"
        />
        @if (editForm.controls.name.invalid && editForm.controls.name.touched) {
          <small class="error-text">El nombre es obligatorio (mÃ­nimo 2 caracteres).</small>
        }
      </div>

      <!-- Capacidad y Precio por noche -->
      <div class="form-row">

          <label for="pricePerNight">Precio por noche *</label>

          />

          }
        </div>
      </div>

      <!-- DescripciÃ³n -->

import { Component, ChangeDetectionStrategy, OnInit, inject, signal } from '@angular/core';
import { CommonModule } from '@angular/common';
import { FormBuilder, ReactiveFormsModule, Validators } from '@angular/forms';
import { ActivatedRoute, Router, RouterLink } from '@angular/router';
import { RoomTypeService } from '../../../core/services/room-type.service';
import { RoomTypeDTORequest } from '../../../models/RoomType-model';

@Component({
  selector: 'app-room-type-edit',
  standalone: true,
  imports: [CommonModule, ReactiveFormsModule, RouterLink],
  templateUrl: './room-type-edit.component.html',
  styleUrl: './room-type-edit.component.css',
  changeDetection: ChangeDetectionStrategy.OnPush
})
export class RoomTypeEditComponent implements OnInit {
  private fb = inject(FormBuilder);
  private route = inject(ActivatedRoute);
  private router = inject(Router);
  private roomTypeService = inject(RoomTypeService);

  roomTypeId = signal<number | null>(null);
  isLoading = signal<boolean>(true);
  isSubmitting = signal<boolean>(false);
  errorMessage = signal<string | null>(null);

  editForm = this.fb.nonNullable.group({
    name: ['', [Validators.required, Validators.minLength(2), Validators.maxLength(100)]],
    capacity: [1, [Validators.required, Validators.min(1), Validators.max(20)]],
    description: [''],
    pricePerNight: [0, [Validators.required, Validators.min(0.01)]]
  });

  ngOnInit(): void {
    const idParam = this.route.snapshot.paramMap.get('id');
    const id = Number(idParam);

    if (!idParam || isNaN(id)) {
      this.errorMessage.set('ID de tipo de habitaciÃ³n invÃ¡lido.');
      this.isLoading.set(false);
      return;
    }

    this.roomTypeId.set(id);
    this.loadRoomType(id);
  }

  private loadRoomType(id: number): void {
    this.roomTypeService.getById(id).subscribe({
      next: (roomType) => {
        this.editForm.patchValue({
          name: roomType.name,
          capacity: roomType.capacity,
          description: roomType.description ?? '',
          pricePerNight: roomType.pricePerNight
        });
        this.isLoading.set(false);
      },
      error: (err) => {
        console.error('Error al cargar tipo de habitaciÃ³n:', err);
        this.errorMessage.set('No se pudo cargar la informaciÃ³n del tipo de habitaciÃ³n.');
        this.isLoading.set(false);
      }
    });
  }

  onSubmit(): void {
    if (this.editForm.invalid || !this.roomTypeId()) {
      this.editForm.markAllAsTouched();
      return;
    }

    this.isSubmitting.set(true);
    this.errorMessage.set(null);

    const formValue = this.editForm.getRawValue();
    const request: RoomTypeDTORequest = {
      name: formValue.name.trim(),
      capacity: Number(formValue.capacity),
      description: formValue.description?.trim() || undefined,
      pricePerNight: Number(formValue.pricePerNight)
    };

    this.roomTypeService.updateRoomType(this.roomTypeId()!, request).subscribe({
      next: () => {
        this.isSubmitting.set(false);
        this.router.navigate(['/dashboard/room-list']); 
      },
      error: (err) => {
        console.error('Error al actualizar tipo de habitaciÃ³n:', err);
        this.errorMessage.set(err?.error?.message || 'Error al actualizar el tipo de habitaciÃ³n.');
        this.isSubmitting.set(false);
      }
    });
  }
}

import { Component, inject, OnInit, signal } from '@angular/core';
import {ReactiveFormsModule, NonNullableFormBuilder, Validators} from '@angular/forms';
import { ActivatedRoute, Router, RouterLink } from '@angular/router';

import { RoomTypeService } from '../../../core/services/room-type.service';
import { RoomTypeDTORequest } from '../../../models/RoomType-model';

@Component({
  selector: 'app-room-type-form',
  standalone: true,
  imports: [ReactiveFormsModule, RouterLink],
  templateUrl: './room-type-form.component.html',
  styleUrl: './room-type-form.component.css'
})
export class RoomTypeFormComponent implements OnInit {

  private fb = inject(NonNullableFormBuilder);
  private roomTypeService = inject(RoomTypeService);
  private router = inject(Router);
  private route = inject(ActivatedRoute);

  isEditMode = signal(false);
  roomTypeId = signal<number | null>(null);

  loading = signal(false);
  saving = signal(false);
  errorMessage = signal('');
  loadError = signal(false);

  roomTypeForm = this.fb.group({
    name: ['', [
      Validators.required,
      Validators.minLength(1),
      Validators.maxLength(50)
    ]],
    capacity: [1, [
      Validators.required,
      Validators.min(1)
    ]],
    description: ['', [
      Validators.required,
      Validators.maxLength(250)
    ]],
    pricePerNight: [0, [
      Validators.required,
      Validators.min(0.01)
    ]]
  });

  ngOnInit(): void { //Detecta si se debe crear o editar un tipo de habitacion
    const idParam = this.route.snapshot.paramMap.get('id');

    if (idParam !== null) {
      const id = Number(idParam);

      if (!Number.isInteger(id) || id <= 0) {
        this.errorMessage.set('El ID del tipo de habitaciÃ³n no es vÃ¡lido.');
        this.loadError.set(true);
        return;
      }

      this.isEditMode.set(true);
      this.roomTypeId.set(id);
      this.loadRoomTypeData(id);
    }
  }

  loadRoomTypeData(id: number): void { //Carga datos de habitacion y crea formulario para su edicion
    this.loading.set(true);
    this.loadError.set(false);
    this.errorMessage.set('');

    this.roomTypeService.getById(id).subscribe({
      next: (data) => {
        this.roomTypeForm.patchValue({
          name: data.name,
          capacity: data.capacity,
          description: data.description ?? '',
          pricePerNight: data.pricePerNight
        });

        this.loading.set(false);
      },
      error: () => {
        this.errorMessage.set('No se pudo cargar la informaciÃ³n del tipo de habitaciÃ³n.');
        this.loadError.set(true);
        this.loading.set(false);
      }
    });
  }

  onSubmit(): void {
    if (this.roomTypeForm.invalid || this.saving() ||
        this.loading() || this.loadError()) {
      this.roomTypeForm.markAllAsTouched();
      return;
    }

    this.saving.set(true);
    this.errorMessage.set('');

    const request: RoomTypeDTORequest = this.roomTypeForm.getRawValue();

    const id = this.roomTypeId();

    if (this.isEditMode() && id !== null) { //Modo edicion
      this.roomTypeService.updateRoomType(id, request).subscribe({
        next: () => {
          this.saving.set(false);
          this.router.navigate(['/dashboard/room-type-list']);
        },
        error: () => {
          this.errorMessage.set('No se pudo actualizar el tipo de habitaciÃ³n.');
          this.saving.set(false);
        }
      });
    } else { //Modo creacion
      this.roomTypeService.createRoomType(request).subscribe({
        next: () => {
          this.saving.set(false);
          this.router.navigate(['/dashboard/room-list']);
        },
        error: () => {
          this.errorMessage.set('No se pudo crear el tipo de habitaciÃ³n. VerificÃ¡ que el nombre no exista previamente.');
          this.saving.set(false);
        }
      });
    }
  }
}

<section class="room-type-list-container">

  <header class="room-type-list-header">
    <h1>GestiÃ³n de tipos de habitaciÃ³n</h1>
    <a
      routerLink="/dashboard/room-type-creation"
      class="link-primary"
    >
      Nuevo tipo de habitaciÃ³n
    </a>
  </header>

  <!-- Error al realizar una acciÃ³n -->
  @if (actionError()) {
    <p class="error-message" role="alert">
      {{ actionError() }}
    </p>
  }

  <!-- Cargando tipos de habitaciÃ³n -->
  @if (loading()) {
    <p>Cargando tipos de habitaciÃ³n...</p>
  }

  <!-- Error al cargar los tipos -->
  @else if (errorMessage()) {
    <div class="error-container">
      <p class="error-message">
        {{ errorMessage() }}
      </p>
      <button
        type="button"
        (click)="loadRoomTypes()"
      >
        Reintentar
      </button>
    </div>
  }
  <!-- No existen tipos de habitaciÃ³n -->
  @else if (roomTypes().length === 0) {
    <p>No hay tipos de habitaciÃ³n registrados.</p>
  }

  <!-- Listado de tipos de habitaciÃ³n -->
  @else {
    <div class="table-container">
      <table class="room-type-table">
        <thead>
          <tr>
            <th>Nombre</th>
            <th>Capacidad</th>
            <th>DescripciÃ³n</th>
            <th>Precio por noche</th>
            <th>Acciones</th>
          </tr>
        </thead>
        <tbody>

          @for (type of roomTypes(); track type.id) {
            <tr>
              <td>{{ type.name }}</td>
              <td>{{ type.capacity }}</td>
              <td>
                {{ type.description || 'Sin descripciÃ³n' }}
              </td>
              <td>
                {{ type.pricePerNight }}
              </td>
              <td class="room-type-actions">
                <a
                  [routerLink]="['/dashboard/room-type-edit', type.id]"
                  class="link-edit"
                >
                  Editar
                </a>
                <button
                  type="button"
                  class="btn-danger"
                  (click)="deleteRoomType(type.id)"
                >
                   Eliminar
                </button>
              </td>
            </tr>
          }
        </tbody>
      </table>
    </div>
  }
</section>

import { Component, inject, OnInit, signal } from '@angular/core';
import { RouterLink } from '@angular/router';

import { RoomTypeService } from '../../../core/services/room-type.service';
import { RoomTypeDTOResponse } from '../../../models/RoomType-model';

@Component({
  selector: 'app-room-type-list',
  standalone: true,
  imports: [RouterLink],
  templateUrl: './room-type-list.component.html',
  styleUrl: './room-type-list.component.css'
})
export class RoomTypeListComponent implements OnInit {

  private roomTypeService = inject(RoomTypeService);

  roomTypes = signal<RoomTypeDTOResponse[]>([]);
  loading = signal(true);
  errorMessage = signal(''); //Para errores al obtener tipos
  actionError = signal(''); //Para errores al eliminar tipos

  ngOnInit(): void {
    this.loadRoomTypes();
  }

  loadRoomTypes(): void {
    this.loading.set(true);
    this.errorMessage.set('');

    this.roomTypeService.getAll().subscribe({
      next: (data) => {
        this.roomTypes.set(data);
        this.loading.set(false);
      },
      error: () => {
        this.errorMessage.set('No se pudieron cargar los tipos de habitaciÃ³n.');
        this.loading.set(false);
      }
    });
  }

  deleteRoomType(id: number): void {
    console.log('Â¡Clic en eliminar detectado para el ID:', id); // <--- Agrega esto
    this.actionError.set('');

    this.roomTypeService.deleteRoomType(id).subscribe({
      next: () => {
        this.roomTypes.update(types => types.filter(type => type.id !== id));
      },
      error: (err) => {
        console.error('Error al eliminar:', err);
        this.actionError.set('No se pudo eliminar el tipo de habitaciÃ³n.');
      }
    });
  }
}

            </option>

        />

        @if (
          attentionForm.controls.quantity.invalid &&
          attentionForm.controls.quantity.touched

          </small>
        }
      </div>

      @if (getSelectedItem(); as selectedItem) {
        <div class="subtotal-info">

            attentionForm.invalid ||

</section>
import {
  Component,
  OnInit,
  inject,
  signal
} from '@angular/core';

import {
  ReactiveFormsModule,
  NonNullableFormBuilder,
  Validators,
  FormsModule
} from '@angular/forms';

import { ActivatedRoute, Router } from '@angular/router';

import { RoomAttentionService } from '../../../core/services/room-attention.service';
import { ItemService } from '../../../core/services/item.service';

import { RoomAttentionDtoRequest } from '../../../models/RoomAttention-model';
import { ItemDtoResponse } from '../../../models/Item-model';

@Component({
  selector: 'app-room-attention-form',
  standalone: true,
  imports: [ReactiveFormsModule, FormsModule],
  templateUrl: './room-attention-form.component.html',
  styleUrl: './room-attention-form.component.css'
})
export class RoomAttentionFormComponent implements OnInit {

  private fb = inject(NonNullableFormBuilder);
  private roomAttentionService = inject(RoomAttentionService);
  private itemService = inject(ItemService);
  private route = inject(ActivatedRoute);
  private router = inject(Router);

  bookingId = signal<number | null>(null);

  availableItems = signal<ItemDtoResponse[]>([]);

  loadingItems = signal(false);
  saving = signal(false);
  errorMessage = signal('');

  attentionForm = this.fb.group({
    itemId: [0, [Validators.required, Validators.min(1)]],
    quantity: [1, [Validators.required, Validators.min(1)]]
  });

  ngOnInit(): void {
    const idParam = this.route.snapshot.paramMap.get('id');
    const id = Number(idParam);

    if (idParam && Number.isInteger(id) && id > 0) {
      this.bookingId.set(id);
      this.loadAvailableItems();
    } else {
      this.errorMessage.set('ID de reserva no vÃ¡lido.');
    }
  }

  // Obtiene catÃ¡logo de productos y servicios
  loadAvailableItems(): void {
    this.loadingItems.set(true);
    this.errorMessage.set('');

    this.itemService.getAll().subscribe({
      next: (items) => {
        this.availableItems.set(items);
        this.loadingItems.set(false);
      },
      error: () => {
        this.errorMessage.set('Error al cargar el catÃ¡logo de productos y servicios.');
        this.loadingItems.set(false);
      }
    });
  }

  // Busca el producto seleccionado
  getSelectedItem(): ItemDtoResponse | undefined {
    const itemId = this.attentionForm.controls.itemId.value;
    return this.availableItems().find(item => item.id === itemId);
  }

  calculateSubtotal(): number {
    const selectedItem = this.getSelectedItem();

    if (!selectedItem) {
      return 0;
    }

    const quantity = this.attentionForm.controls.quantity.value;
    return selectedItem.unitPrice * quantity;
  }

  // Registra un producto o servicio consumido por un huÃ©sped durante su reserva
  onSubmit(): void {
    if (this.attentionForm.invalid) {
      this.attentionForm.markAllAsTouched();
      return;
    }

    const id = this.bookingId();

    if (id === null || this.saving()) {
      return;
    }

    const formValue = this.attentionForm.getRawValue();

    const request: RoomAttentionDtoRequest = {
      bookingId: id,
      itemId: formValue.itemId,
      quantity: formValue.quantity
    };

    this.saving.set(true);
    this.errorMessage.set('');

    this.roomAttentionService.addAttention(request).subscribe({
      next: () => {
        this.saving.set(false);
        this.router.navigate(['/dashboard/bookings', id, 'servicios']);
      },
      error: () => {
        this.errorMessage.set('Error al registrar el consumo o servicio.');
        this.saving.set(false);
      }
    });
  }
}

import { Component, OnInit, computed, inject, signal } from '@angular/core';
import { ActivatedRoute, RouterLink } from '@angular/router';
import { CommonModule } from '@angular/common';

import { RoomAttentionService } from '../../../core/services/room-attention.service';
import { RoomAttentionDtoResponse } from '../../../models/RoomAttention-model';

@Component({
  selector: 'app-room-attention-list',
  standalone: true,
  imports: [RouterLink, CommonModule],
  templateUrl: './room-attention-list.component.html',
  styleUrl: './room-attention-list.component.css'
})
export class RoomAttentionListComponent implements OnInit {

  private roomAttentionService = inject(RoomAttentionService);
  private route = inject(ActivatedRoute);

  bookingId = signal<number | null>(null);
  attentions = signal<RoomAttentionDtoResponse[]>([]);

  loading = signal<boolean>(false);
  deletingId = signal<number | null>(null);
  deliveringId = signal<number | null>(null);

  errorMessage = signal<string>('');
  canModify = signal<boolean>(false);

  total = computed(() =>
    this.attentions().reduce(
      (acc, attention) => acc + (attention.subtotal || 0),
      0
    )
  );

  // Al iniciar el componente, valido los permisos del usuario y obtengo el ID de la reserva desde la ruta
  ngOnInit(): void {
    this.checkUserRole();

    const idParam = this.route.snapshot.paramMap.get('id');
    const id = Number(idParam);

    if (idParam && Number.isInteger(id) && id > 0) {
      this.bookingId.set(id);
      this.loadAttentions();
    } else {
      this.errorMessage.set('ID de reserva no vÃ¡lido.');
    }
  }

  // Verifico los roles almacenados en el navegador para definir si el usuario puede modificar o eliminar registros
  checkUserRole(): void {
    const storedRoles = localStorage.getItem('role');

    if (!storedRoles) {
      this.canModify.set(false);
      return;
    }

    let roles: unknown;

    try {
      roles = JSON.parse(storedRoles);
    } catch {
      roles = storedRoles;
    }

    const roleList = Array.isArray(roles) ? roles : [roles];

    const allowed = roleList.some((role: unknown) => {
      let value = '';

      if (typeof role === 'string') {
        value = role;
      } else if (role && typeof role === 'object' && 'authority' in role) {
        value = String((role as any).authority);
      }

      return [
        'ADMIN',
        'ROLE_ADMIN',
        'RECEPCIONIST',
        'ROLE_RECEPCIONIST'
      ].includes(value);
    });

    this.canModify.set(allowed);
  }

  // Invoco al servicio para obtener la lista de consumos asociados a la reserva actual
  loadAttentions(): void {
    const id = this.bookingId();

    if (id === null) return;

    this.loading.set(true);
    this.errorMessage.set('');

    this.roomAttentionService.getByBooking(id).subscribe({
      next: (data: RoomAttentionDtoResponse[]) => {
        this.attentions.set(data);
        this.loading.set(false);
      },
      error: () => {
        this.errorMessage.set('Error al cargar los consumos de la reserva.');
        this.loading.set(false);
      }
    });
  }

  // Valido permisos y elimino un consumo especÃ­fico a travÃ©s del servicio
  deleteAttention(id: number): void {
    if (!this.canModify() || this.deletingId() !== null) return;

    this.deletingId.set(id);
    this.errorMessage.set('');

    this.roomAttentionService.deleteAttention(id).subscribe({
      next: () => {
        this.attentions.update(attentions =>
          attentions.filter(attention => attention.id !== id)
        );
        this.deletingId.set(null);
      },
      error: () => {
        this.errorMessage.set('Error al eliminar el consumo.');
        this.deletingId.set(null);
      }
    });
  }

  // Valido permisos y marco un consumo como entregado mediante el servicio
  markAsDelivered(id: number): void {
    if (!this.canModify() || this.deliveringId() !== null) return;

    this.deliveringId.set(id);
    this.errorMessage.set('');

    this.roomAttentionService.markAsDelivered(id).subscribe({
      next: () => {
        this.deliveringId.set(null);
        this.loadAttentions();
      },
      error: () => {
        this.errorMessage.set('Error al marcar el consumo como entregado.');
        this.deliveringId.set(null);
      }
    });
  }
}

  <div class="header-section">
    <h2>
      <i class="fa-solid fa-bell-concierge" style="margin-right: 0.5rem;"></i> Servicio al Cuarto (GestiÃ³n Global)
    </h2>
  </div>

  <hr class="divider" />

  <!-- Mensaje de Error -->
  @if (errorMessage()) {
    <div class="alert alert-danger">
      <i class="fa-solid fa-triangle-exclamation" style="margin-right: 0.5rem;"></i> {{ errorMessage() }}
    </div>
  }

  <!-- Estado de Carga -->
  @if (loading()) {
    <div class="alert alert-info">
      <i class="fa-solid fa-rotate spin" style="margin-right: 0.5rem;"></i> Cargando solicitudes de servicio al cuarto...
    </div>
  } @else {
    @if (attentions().length === 0) {
      <div class="alert alert-info">
        <i class="fa-solid fa-circle-info" style="margin-right: 0.5rem;"></i> No hay solicitudes de servicio al cuarto registradas en el sistema.
      </div>
    } @else {
      <!-- Contenedor con Estilo Glass para la Tabla -->
      <div class="glass-table-container">
        <div class="table-responsive">
          <table class="table-glass">
            <thead>
              <tr>
                <th>ID</th>
                <th>HabitaciÃ³n / Reserva</th>
                <th>DescripciÃ³n</th>
                <th>Cant.</th>
                <th>Subtotal</th>
                <th>Registrado por</th>
                <th>Fecha / Hora</th>
                <th>Estado</th>
                <th style="text-align: right;">Acciones</th>
              </tr>
            </thead>
            <tbody>
              @for (item of attentions(); track item.id) {
                <tr [class.row-pending]="!item.delivered" [class.row-delivered]="item.delivered">
                  <td><strong class="text-subtitle">#{{ item.id }}</strong></td>
                  <td>
                    <div style="display: flex; flex-direction: column; gap: 0.25rem;">
                      <span class="badge" style="background: rgba(129, 140, 248, 0.2); color: #818cf8; font-weight: 600;">
                        <i class="fa-solid fa-door-open" style="margin-right: 0.25rem;"></i> Hab. {{ item.roomNumber || $any(item).bookingEntity?.room?.number || $any(item).room || 'N/A' }}
                      </span>
                      <span class="text-subtitle" style="font-size: 0.75rem;">Reserva #{{ item.bookingId }}</span>
                    </div>
                  </td>
                  <td>
                    <div class="text-main" style="font-weight: 500;">
                      {{ item.itemDTOResponse.description || 'Consumo #' + item.id }}
                    </div>
                  </td>
                  <td>
                    <span class="text-main">{{ item.quantity }}</span>
                  </td>
                  <td>
                    <span class="text-main" style="font-weight: bold; color: #4ade80;">${{ item.subtotal | number:'1.2-2' }}</span>
                  </td>
                  <td>
                    <span class="badge badge-secondary" style="font-size: 0.8rem;">
                      <i class="fa-solid fa-user" style="margin-right: 0.25rem;"></i> {{ item.employeeUsername || 'Sistema' }}
                    </span>
                  </td>
                  <td>
                    <span class="text-subtitle" style="font-size: 0.85rem;">{{ item.createdAt || 'N/A' }}</span>
                  </td>
                  <td>
                    @if (item.delivered) {
                      <span class="badge badge-success-glass">
                        <i class="fa-solid fa-circle-check" style="margin-right: 0.25rem;"></i> Entregado
                      </span>
                    } @else {
                      <span class="badge badge-warning-glass" style="background: rgba(251, 191, 36, 0.2); color: #fbbf24;">
                        <i class="fa-solid fa-clock" style="margin-right: 0.25rem;"></i> Pendiente
                      </span>
                    }
                  </td>
                  
                  <!-- Acciones de Entregar / Eliminar -->
                  <td style="text-align: right;">
                    <div style="display: flex; justify-content: flex-end; gap: 0.5rem;">
                      @if (!item.delivered) {
                        <button 
                          type="button"
                          class="btn-secondary-action" 
                          [disabled]="deliveringId() === item.id"
                          (click)="markAsDelivered(item.id)">
                          <i class="fa-solid fa-check-double" style="margin-right: 0.25rem;"></i> Entregar
                        </button>
                      }
                      <button 
                        type="button"
                        class="btn-danger-action" 
                        [disabled]="deletingId() === item.id"
                        (click)="deleteAttention(item.id)">
                          <i class="fa-solid fa-trash" style="margin-right: 0.25rem;"></i> Eliminar
                      </button>
                    </div>
                  </td>
                </tr>
              }
            </tbody>
          </table>
        </div>
      </div>
    }
  }

  <!-- BotÃ³n Volver -->
  <div style="margin-top: 1.5rem;">
    <a routerLink="/dashboard" class="btn-secondary-action">
      <i class="fa-solid fa-arrow-left" style="margin-right: 0.25rem;"></i> Volver al Inicio
    </a>
  </div>
</div>
import { Component, OnInit, ChangeDetectionStrategy, signal, inject } from '@angular/core';
import { CommonModule } from '@angular/common';
import { RouterModule } from '@angular/router';
import { RoomAttentionService } from '../../core/services/room-attention.service';
import { BookingService } from '../../core/services/booking.service';
import { RoomAttentionDtoResponse } from '../../models/RoomAttention-model';
import { forkJoin, of } from 'rxjs';
import { catchError } from 'rxjs/operators';

@Component({
  selector: 'app-room-attention-global-list',
  standalone: true,
  imports: [CommonModule, RouterModule],
  templateUrl: './room-attention-global-list.component.html',
  styleUrls: ['./room-attention-global-list.component.css'],
  changeDetection: ChangeDetectionStrategy.OnPush
})
export class RoomAttentionGlobalListComponent implements OnInit {
  attentions = signal<(RoomAttentionDtoResponse & { roomNumber?: string })[]>([]);
  loading = signal<boolean>(true);
  errorMessage = signal<string>('');
  deliveringId = signal<number | null>(null);
  deletingId = signal<number | null>(null);

  private roomAttentionService = inject(RoomAttentionService);
  private bookingService = inject(BookingService);

  ngOnInit(): void {
    this.loadAllAttentions();
  }

  // Traigo en paralelo los consumos y las reservas para unificar la informaciÃ³n y ordenar la lista
  loadAllAttentions(): void {
    this.loading.set(true);
    this.errorMessage.set('');

    forkJoin({
      attentions: this.roomAttentionService.getAll(),
      bookings: this.bookingService.getAll().pipe(catchError(() => of([])))
    }).subscribe({
      next: ({ attentions, bookings }) => {
        // Mapeo las reservas para asociar cada ID con su respectivo nÃºmero de habitaciÃ³n
        const roomMap = new Map<number, string>();
        bookings.forEach((b: any) => {
          const roomNum = b.roomNumber || b.room?.number || b.roomEntity?.number || 'N/A';
          roomMap.set(b.id, roomNum);
        });

        // Enriquezco los Ã­tems de atenciÃ³n agregando el nÃºmero de habitaciÃ³n correspondiente
        const enriched = attentions.map(item => ({
          ...item,
          roomNumber: roomMap.get(item.bookingId) || 'N/A'
        }));

        // Ordeno los elementos colocando los pendientes primero y luego por fecha descendente
        const sorted = enriched.sort((a, b) => {
          if (a.delivered === b.delivered) {
            return new Date(b.createdAt || 0).getTime() - new Date(a.createdAt || 0).getTime();
          }
          return a.delivered ? 1 : -1;
        });
        
        this.attentions.set(sorted);
        this.loading.set(false);
      },
      error: (err) => {
        console.error('Error al cargar consumos o reservas:', err);
        this.errorMessage.set('No se pudieron cargar los consumos de servicio al cuarto.');
        this.loading.set(false);
      }
    });
  }

  // Actualizo el estado del consumo a entregado mediante el servicio
  markAsDelivered(id: number): void {
    this.deliveringId.set(id);
    this.roomAttentionService.markAsDelivered(id).subscribe({
      next: () => {
        this.deliveringId.set(null);
        this.loadAllAttentions();
      },
      error: (err) => {
        console.error('Error al marcar como entregado:', err);
        this.deliveringId.set(null);
      }
    });
  }

  // Solicito confirmaciÃ³n y elimino el registro de consumo a travÃ©s del servicio
  deleteAttention(id: number): void {
    if (confirm('Â¿EstÃ¡s seguro de eliminar este consumo?')) {
      this.deletingId.set(id);
      this.roomAttentionService.deleteAttention(id).subscribe({
        next: () => {
          this.deletingId.set(null);
          this.loadAllAttentions();
        },
        error: (err) => {
          console.error('Error al eliminar consumo:', err);
          this.deletingId.set(null);
        }
      });
    }
  }
}

import { Component, OnInit, ChangeDetectorRef } from '@angular/core';
import { CommonModule } from '@angular/common';
import { FormsModule } from '@angular/forms';
import { Router, RouterLink, ActivatedRoute } from '@angular/router';
import { UserService } from '../../core/services/user.service';
import { RoleType, UserDtoRequest } from '../../models/User-model';

@Component({
  selector: 'app-user-edit',
  standalone: true,
  imports: [CommonModule, FormsModule, RouterLink],
  templateUrl: './user-edit.component.html',
  styleUrls: ['../user-form/user-form.component.css'] 
})
export class UserEditComponent implements OnInit {
  userId: number | null = null;

  formData = {
    username: '',
    name: '',
    surname: '',
    dni: '',
    gender: '',
    email: '',
    phoneNumber: '',
    address: '',
    birthDay: '',
    profilePictureFile: undefined as File | undefined,
    role: 'GUEST' as RoleType,
    enabled: true
  };

  loading = false;
  errorMessage = '';

  constructor(
    private userService: UserService,
    private router: Router,
    private route: ActivatedRoute,
    private cdr: ChangeDetectorRef
  ) {}

  // Al iniciar el componente, leo el parÃ¡metro ID de la URL y cargo los datos del usuario
  ngOnInit(): void {
    const idParam = this.route.snapshot.paramMap.get('id');
    if (idParam) {
      this.userId = Number(idParam);
      this.loadUserData(this.userId);
    }
  }

  // Invoco al servicio para obtener la informaciÃ³n del usuario por su ID y rellenar el formulario
  loadUserData(id: number): void {
    this.loading = true;
    
    this.userService.getById(id).subscribe({
      next: (user: any) => {
        this.formData.username = user.username || '';
        this.formData.name = user.name || '';
        this.formData.surname = user.surname || '';
        this.formData.dni = user.dni || '';
        this.formData.gender = user.gender || '';
        this.formData.email = user.email || '';
        this.formData.phoneNumber = user.phoneNumber || '';
        this.formData.address = user.address || '';
        this.formData.birthDay = user.birthDay || '';
        this.formData.role = user.role || 'GUEST';
        this.formData.enabled = user.enabled ?? true;

        this.loading = false;
        this.cdr.markForCheck();
      },
      error: (err) => {
        this.loading = false;
        this.errorMessage = 'No se pudieron cargar los datos del usuario.';
        this.cdr.markForCheck();
        console.error(err);
      }
    });
  }

  // Capturo el archivo de imagen seleccionado en el input de tipo file
  onFileSelected(event: any): void {
    const file: File = event.target.files[0];
    if (file) {
      this.formData.profilePictureFile = file;
    }
  }

  // Preparo el payload de actualizaciÃ³n y envÃ­o los datos modificados al backend
  onSubmit(): void {
    if (!this.userId) return;

    this.errorMessage = '';
    this.loading = true;

    const updatePayload = {
      username: this.formData.username,
      name: this.formData.name,
      surname: this.formData.surname,
      dni: this.formData.dni,
      gender: this.formData.gender || undefined,
      email: this.formData.email,
      phoneNumber: this.formData.phoneNumber || undefined,
      address: this.formData.address || undefined,
      birthDay: this.formData.birthDay ? this.formData.birthDay : ''
    };

    // Actualizo los datos de texto del usuario mediante el servicio
    this.userService.updateUser(this.userId, updatePayload).subscribe({
      next: () => {
        // Si se seleccionÃ³ una nueva foto de perfil, la subo a continuaciÃ³n
        if (this.formData.profilePictureFile && this.userId) {
          this.onUploadPhoto(this.userId, this.formData.profilePictureFile);
        } else {
          this.loading = false;
          alert('Â¡Usuario modificado con Ã©xito!');
          this.router.navigate(['/dashboard/usuarios']);
        }
      },
      error: (err: any) => {
        this.loading = false;
        this.errorMessage = err.error?.mensaje || err.error?.message || 'Error al actualizar el usuario.';
        this.cdr.markForCheck();
        alert(this.errorMessage);
        console.error(err);
      }
    });
  }

  // EnvÃ­o la nueva imagen de perfil al servidor asociada al ID del usuario
  onUploadPhoto(userId: number, file: File): void {
    const formData = new FormData();
    formData.append('profilePicture', file);

    this.userService.updateProfilePicture(userId, formData).subscribe({
      next: (response: any) => {
        this.loading = false;
        alert('Â¡Usuario y foto de perfil modificados con Ã©xito!');
        this.router.navigate(['/dashboard/usuarios']);
      },
      error: (err: any) => {
        this.loading = false;
        console.error('Error al actualizar la foto', err);
        alert('Se guardaron los datos, pero hubo un error al actualizar la foto de perfil.');
        this.router.navigate(['/dashboard/usuarios']);
      }
    });
  }
}

        <input type="text" id="name" class="input-field" name="name" 
               [value]="formData.name" 
               (input)="formData.name = $any($event.target).value" 
               required placeholder="Juan">
      </div>

      <div class="field">
        <label for="surname" class="label">Apellido *</label>
        <input type="text" id="surname" class="input-field" name="surname" 
               [value]="formData.surname" 
               (input)="formData.surname = $any($event.target).value" 
               required placeholder="PÃ©rez">
      </div>

      <div class="field">
        <label for="dni" class="label">DNI / NÃºmero de IdentificaciÃ³n *</label>
        <input type="text" id="dni" class="input-field" name="dni" 
               [value]="formData.dni" 
               (input)="formData.dni = $any($event.target).value" 
               required placeholder="12345678">
      </div>

      <div class="field">
        <label for="gender" class="label">GÃ©nero</label>
        <select id="gender" class="input-field" name="gender" 
                [value]="formData.gender" 
                (change)="formData.gender = $any($event.target).value">
          <option value="" disabled selected>Seleccione gÃ©nero</option>
          <option value="Masculino">Masculino</option>
          <option value="Femenino">Femenino</option>
          <option value="Otro">Otro</option>
        </select>
      </div>

      <div class="field">
        <label for="birthDay" class="label">Fecha de Nacimiento</label>
        <input type="date" id="birthDay" class="input-field" name="birthDay" 
               [value]="formData.birthDay" 
               (input)="formData.birthDay = $any($event.target).value">
      </div>
    </div>

    <hr class="section-divider">

    <!-- SecciÃ³n de Datos de Contacto -->
    <h4 class="section-title">Datos de Contacto</h4>
    <div class="form-grid">
      <div class="field">

               (input)="formData.email = $any($event.target).value" 
               required placeholder="juan@example.com">
      </div>

      <div class="field">
        <label for="phoneNumber" class="label">TelÃ©fono *</label>

               required placeholder="+54 9 11...">
      </div>

      <div class="field">
        <label for="address" class="label">DirecciÃ³n</label>
        <input type="text" id="address" class="input-field" name="address"

               (input)="formData.address = $any($event.target).value" 
               placeholder="Calle Falsa 123">
      </div>

      <div class="field switch-field">
        <div class="switch-wrapper">
          <input class="switch-input" type="checkbox" id="enabled" name="enabled" 
                 [checked]="formData.enabled" 
                 (change)="formData.enabled = $any($event.target).checked">
          <label class="switch-label" for="enabled">Usuario activo</label>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>

    <hr class="section-divider">

    <!-- SecciÃ³n de Foto de Perfil -->

        Cancelar

      </button>

  </form>

import { Component, ChangeDetectorRef } from '@angular/core';
import { CommonModule } from '@angular/common';
import { FormsModule } from '@angular/forms';
import { Router, RouterLink } from '@angular/router';
import { UserService } from '../../core/services/user.service';
import { UserDtoRequestCreation, RoleType } from '../../models/User-model';

@Component({
  selector: 'app-user-form',
  standalone: true,
  imports: [CommonModule, FormsModule, RouterLink],
  templateUrl: './user-form.component.html',
  styleUrls: ['./user-form.component.css']
})
export class UserFormComponent {
  formData = {
    username: '',
    password: '',
    name: '',
    surname: '',
    dni: '',
    gender: '',
    email: '',
    phoneNumber: '',
    address: '',
    birthDay: '',
    role: 'GUEST' as RoleType,
    profilePictureFile: undefined as File | undefined,
    enabled: true
  };

  loading = false;
  errorMessage = '';

  constructor(
    private userService: UserService,
    private router: Router,
    private cdr: ChangeDetectorRef
  ) {}

  // Capturo el archivo seleccionado desde el input de tipo file para la foto de perfil
  onFileSelected(event: any): void {
    const file: File = event.target.files[0];
    if (file) {
      this.formData.profilePictureFile = file;
    }
  }

  // Preparo el payload con los datos del formulario e invoco al servicio para registrar el usuario
  onSubmit(): void {
    this.errorMessage = '';
    this.loading = true;

    const payload: UserDtoRequestCreation = {
      username: this.formData.username,
      password: this.formData.password,
      name: this.formData.name,
      surname: this.formData.surname,
      dni: this.formData.dni,
      gender: this.formData.gender,
      email: this.formData.email,
      phoneNumber: this.formData.phoneNumber,
      address: this.formData.address || undefined,
      birthDay: this.formData.birthDay ? this.formData.birthDay : undefined,
      role: this.formData.role,
      profilePictureFile: this.formData.profilePictureFile,
      enabled: this.formData.enabled
    };

    // EnvÃ­o el objeto de transferencia al backend mediante el servicio HTTP
    this.userService.createUser(payload).subscribe({
      next: () => {
        this.loading = false;
        alert('Â¡Usuario creado con Ã©xito!');
        this.router.navigate(['/dashboard/usuarios']);
      },
      error: (err) => {
        this.loading = false;
        this.errorMessage = err.error?.mensaje || err.error?.message || 'Error al registrar el usuario en el sistema. Verifique los datos o si el username/email/DNI ya existen.';
        this.cdr.markForCheck();
        alert(this.errorMessage);
        console.error(err);
      }
    });
  }
}

          </div>

      </div>

  }

// src/app/components/user-list/user-list.component.ts
import { Component, OnInit, ChangeDetectorRef } from '@angular/core';
import { CommonModule } from '@angular/common';
import { Router, RouterLink } from '@angular/router';
import { UserService } from '../../core/services/user.service';
import { UserDtoResponse } from '../../models/User-model';
import { AuthService } from '../../core/services/auth.service';

@Component({
  selector: 'app-user-list',
  standalone: true,
  imports: [CommonModule, RouterLink],
  templateUrl: './user-list.component.html',
  styleUrls: ['./user-list.component.css']
})
export class UserListComponent implements OnInit {
  users: UserDtoResponse[] = [];
  loading = true;
  errorMessage = '';
  canModify = false;
  userAvatars: { [key: number]: string } = {};
  selectedUser: UserDtoResponse | null = null;

  constructor(
    private userService: UserService,
    private authService: AuthService,
    private router: Router,
    private cdr: ChangeDetectorRef
  ) {}

  ngOnInit(): void {
    this.checkUserRole();
    this.loadUsers();
  }

  // Valido el rol almacenado en el localStorage para definir permisos de modificaciÃ³n
  checkUserRole(): void {
    const storedRoles = localStorage.getItem('role');
    if (!storedRoles) {
      this.canModify = false;
      return;
    }

    try {
      const roles = JSON.parse(storedRoles);
      const rolesArray = Array.isArray(roles) ? roles : [roles];
      
      this.canModify = rolesArray.some((r: any) => {
        const roleName = typeof r === 'string' ? r : (r?.name || '');
        return roleName === 'ROLE_ADMIN' || roleName === 'ROLE_RECEPCIONIST';
      });
    } catch (e) {
      this.canModify = false;
    }
  }

  // Invoco al servicio para obtener la lista de usuarios y precargar sus avatares
  loadUsers(): void {
    this.loading = true;
    this.errorMessage = '';
    
    this.userService.getByRole('GUEST').subscribe({
      next: (data) => {
        this.users = Array.isArray(data) ? data : [];
        this.loading = false;
        
        this.users.forEach(user => {
          this.loadUserAvatar(user);
        });

        this.cdr.markForCheck();
      },
      error: (err) => {
        this.errorMessage = 'No se pudieron cargar los huÃ©spedes.';
        this.loading = false;
        this.cdr.markForCheck();
        console.error(err);
      }
    });
  }

  // Verifico permisos y elimino el usuario seleccionado mediante el servicio
  deleteUser(id: number): void {
    if (!this.canModify) {
      alert('No tienes los permisos necesarios para realizar esta acciÃ³n.');
      return;
    }

    if (confirm('Â¿EstÃ¡s seguro de que deseas eliminar este usuario?')) {
      this.userService.deleteUser(id).subscribe({
        next: () => {
          this.users = this.users.filter(user => user.id !== id);
          delete this.userAvatars[id];
          this.cdr.markForCheck();
        },
        error: (err) => {
          alert('Error al eliminar el usuario.');
          console.error(err);
        }
      });
    }
  }

  goToNewUser(): void {
    if (this.canModify) {
      this.router.navigate(['/dashboard/user-creation']);
    } else {
      alert('No tienes los permisos necesarios.');
    }
  }

  openUserDetail(user: UserDtoResponse): void {
    this.selectedUser = user;
    this.loadUserAvatar(user);
  }

  closeUserDetail(): void {
    this.selectedUser = null;
  }

  deleteUserModal(id: number): void {
    this.closeUserDetail();
    this.deleteUser(id);
  }

  // Solicito la imagen al servicio, genero un blob URL local y actualizo el estado
  loadUserAvatar(user: UserDtoResponse): void {
    if (!user || !user.id) return;
    if (this.userAvatars[user.id]) return;

    this.userService.getProfilePicture(user.id).subscribe({
      next: (blob) => {
        const objectURL = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
        this.userAvatars[user.id] = objectURL;
        this.cdr.markForCheck();
      },
      error: (err) => {
        console.error('Error al cargar la foto de perfil', err);
        this.userAvatars[user.id] = 'assets/default-avatar.png';
        this.cdr.markForCheck();
      }
    });
  }

  getUserAvatarUrl(user: UserDtoResponse | null): string {
    if (!user || !user.id) {
      return 'assets/default-avatar.png';
    }
    return this.userAvatars[user.id] || 'assets/default-avatar.png';
  }
}
import { CanActivateFn, Router } from '@angular/router';
import { inject } from '@angular/core';

export const authGuard: CanActivateFn = (route, state) => {
  const router = inject(Router);
  const token = localStorage.getItem('authToken');

  if (token) {
    return true;
  } else {
    router.navigate(['/login']);
    return false;
  }
};
import { CanActivateFn, Router } from '@angular/router';
import { inject } from '@angular/core';

export const noGuestGuard: CanActivateFn = (route, state) => {
  const router = inject(Router);
  
  // Obtenemos el rol guardado en localStorage al iniciar sesiÃ³n
  const userRole = localStorage.getItem('role') || '';
  const isGuest = userRole.toUpperCase().includes('GUEST');

  if (isGuest) {
    // Si es guest, lo mandamos automÃ¡ticamente a la lista de reservas
    router.navigate(['/dashboard/booking-list']);
    return false;
  }

  // Si es admin o empleado, se le permite ver el inicio
  return true;
};
import { HttpInterceptorFn, HttpErrorResponse } from '@angular/common/http';
import { inject } from '@angular/core';
import { AuthService } from '../services/auth.service';
import { catchError, switchMap, throwError } from 'rxjs';

let isRefreshing = false;

export const authInterceptor: HttpInterceptorFn = (req, next) => {
  const authService = inject(AuthService);
  const token = localStorage.getItem('authToken');

  let clonedReq = req;
  if (token) {
    clonedReq = req.clone({
      setHeaders: {
        Authorization: `Bearer ${token}`
      }
    });
  }

  return next(clonedReq).pipe(
    catchError((error: HttpErrorResponse) => {
      if (error.status === 401 && !req.url.includes('/public/auth/refresh') && !req.url.includes('/public/auth/login')) {
        
        if (!isRefreshing) {
          isRefreshing = true;

          return authService.refreshToken().pipe(
            switchMap((response: any) => {
              isRefreshing = false;
              
              
              const newToken = response.accessToken; 
              localStorage.setItem('authToken', newToken);

              const retryReq = req.clone({
                setHeaders: {
                  Authorization: `Bearer ${newToken}`
                }
              });
              return next(retryReq);
            }),
            catchError((refreshError) => {
              isRefreshing = false;
              localStorage.removeItem('authToken');
              return throwError(() => refreshError);
            })
          );
        }
      }

      return throwError(() => error);
    })
  );
};
import { Injectable } from '@angular/core';
import { HttpClient, HttpHeaders } from '@angular/common/http';
import { Observable } from 'rxjs';
import { AccountDTOResponse } from '../../models/Account-model';
import { PaymentDTORequest, PaymentDTOResponse } from '../../models/Payment-model';

@Injectable({
  providedIn: 'root'
})
export class AccountService {
  private apiUrl = 'http://localhost:8080/private/accounts';

  constructor(private http: HttpClient) {}

  // FunciÃ³n privada para generar los headers con el token JWT actual
  private getHeaders(): HttpHeaders {
    const token = localStorage.getItem('authToken') || '';
    return new HttpHeaders({
      'Content-Type': 'application/json',
      'Authorization': `Bearer ${token}`
    });
  }

  getAllAccounts(): Observable<AccountDTOResponse[]> {
    return this.http.get<AccountDTOResponse[]>(this.apiUrl, { 
      headers: this.getHeaders(),
      withCredentials: true 
    });
  }

  getAccountById(id: number): Observable<AccountDTOResponse> {
    return this.http.get<AccountDTOResponse>(`${this.apiUrl}/${id}`, { 
      headers: this.getHeaders(),
      withCredentials: true 
    });
  }

  getAccountByBookingId(bookingId: number): Observable<AccountDTOResponse> {
    return this.http.get<AccountDTOResponse>(`${this.apiUrl}/booking/${bookingId}`, { 
      headers: this.getHeaders(),
      withCredentials: true 
    });
  }

  // MÃ©todo alias para resolver la llamada desde AccountDetailComponent
  getAccountByCheckInId(checkInId: number): Observable<AccountDTOResponse> {
    return this.getAccountByBookingId(checkInId);
  }

  updateAdjustmentPercentage(bookingId: number, adjustmentPercentage: number): Observable<AccountDTOResponse> {
    return this.http.put<AccountDTOResponse>(
      `${this.apiUrl}/booking/${bookingId}/adjustment`, 
      { adjustmentPercentage }, 
      { 
        headers: this.getHeaders(),
        withCredentials: true 
      }
    );
  }

  addPayment(request: PaymentDTORequest): Observable<PaymentDTOResponse> {
    return this.http.post<PaymentDTOResponse>(`${this.apiUrl}/payments`, request, { 
      headers: this.getHeaders(),
      withCredentials: true 
    });
  }

  getPaymentsByBookingId(bookingId: number): Observable<PaymentDTOResponse[]> {
    return this.http.get<PaymentDTOResponse[]>(`${this.apiUrl}/bookings/${bookingId}/payments`, { 
      headers: this.getHeaders(),
      withCredentials: true 
    });
  }
}
import { Injectable, signal, computed, effect } from '@angular/core';
import { HttpClient } from '@angular/common/http';
import { Observable, tap } from 'rxjs';
import { UserLoginDtoRequest, AuthTokenResponse } from '../../models/Auth-model';
import { UserDtoRequest } from '../../models/User-model';

@Injectable({
  providedIn: 'root'
})
export class AuthService {
  private apiUrl = 'http://localhost:8080/public/auth';

  // --- SEÃ‘ALES DE ESTADO ---
  public token = signal<string | null>(localStorage.getItem('authToken'));
  public roles = signal<string[]>(this.getInitialRoles());

  // --- SEÃ‘ALES COMPUTADAS (Soportan con o sin prefijo ROLE_) ---
  public estaLogueado = computed(() => !!this.token());
  
  public isAdmin = computed(() => 
    this.roles().some(r => {
      const upper = r.toUpperCase();
      return upper === 'ADMIN' || upper === 'ROLE_ADMIN';
    })
  );
  
  public isGuest = computed(() => 
    this.roles().some(r => {
      const upper = r.toUpperCase();
      return upper === 'GUEST' || upper === 'ROLE_GUEST';
    })
  );

  constructor(private http: HttpClient) {
    effect(() => {
      console.log('--- Estado de AutenticaciÃ³n Actualizado ---');
      console.log('Â¿EstÃ¡ logueado?:', this.estaLogueado());
      console.log('Â¿Es Admin?:', this.isAdmin());
      console.log('Â¿Es Guest?:', this.isGuest());
    });
  }

  private getInitialRoles(): string[] {
    try {
      const stored = localStorage.getItem('role');
      return stored ? JSON.parse(stored) : [];
    } catch {
      return [];
    }
  }

  public updateSession(token: string, roles: string[]): void {
    localStorage.setItem('authToken', token);
    localStorage.setItem('role', JSON.stringify(roles));
    this.token.set(token);
    this.roles.set(roles);
  }

  public clearSession(): void {
    localStorage.removeItem('authToken');
    localStorage.removeItem('role');
    localStorage.removeItem('userId');
    localStorage.removeItem('username');
    localStorage.removeItem('name');
    localStorage.removeItem('surname');
    this.token.set(null);
    this.roles.set([]);
  }

  login(credentials: UserLoginDtoRequest): Observable<AuthTokenResponse> {
    return this.http.post<AuthTokenResponse>(`${this.apiUrl}/login`, credentials, { withCredentials: true }).pipe(
      tap(response => {
        if (response && response.token) {
          try {
            const payloadBase64 = response.token.split('.')[1];
            const decodedToken = JSON.parse(atob(payloadBase64));
            const roles = decodedToken.roles || [];
            this.updateSession(response.token, roles);
          } catch (e) {
            console.error('Error procesando token en login:', e);
          }
        }
      })
    );
  }


  register(request: any): Observable<AuthTokenResponse> {
    const formData = new FormData();

    Object.keys(request).forEach(key => {
      const value = request[key];
      if (value !== null && value !== undefined && value !== '') {
        if (key === 'profilePictureFile' && value instanceof File) {
          formData.append(key, value, value.name);
        } else {
          formData.append(key, value.toString());
        }
      }
    });

    return this.http.post<AuthTokenResponse>(`${this.apiUrl}/register`, formData, { withCredentials: true }).pipe(
      tap(response => {
        if (response && response.token) {
          try {
            const payloadBase64 = response.token.split('.')[1];
            const decodedToken = JSON.parse(atob(payloadBase64));
            const roles = decodedToken.roles || [];
            this.updateSession(response.token, roles);
          } catch (e) {
            console.error('Error procesando token en registro:', e);
          }
        }
      })
    );
  }

  logout(): Observable<void> {
    return this.http.post<void>(`${this.apiUrl}/logout`, {}, { withCredentials: true }).pipe(
      tap(() => {
        this.clearSession();
      })
    );
  }

  refreshToken(): Observable<AuthTokenResponse> {
    return this.http.post<AuthTokenResponse>(`${this.apiUrl}/refresh`, {}, { withCredentials: true }).pipe(
      tap(response => {
        if (response && response.token) {
          try {
            const payloadBase64 = response.token.split('.')[1];
            const decodedToken = JSON.parse(atob(payloadBase64));
            const roles = decodedToken.roles || [];
            this.updateSession(response.token, roles);
          } catch (e) {
            console.error('Error procesando token en refresh:', e);
          }
        }
      })
    );
  }

  getProfilePictureUrl(userId: number): string {
    return `${this.apiUrl}/${userId}/profile-picture`;
  }

  getUserIdByUsername(username: string): Observable<number> {
    return this.http.get<number>(`${this.apiUrl}/by-username/${username}`);
  }
}

    return this.http.post<void>(`${this.apiUrl}/process-no-shows`, {}, { withCredentials: true });
  }

import { Injectable } from '@angular/core';
import { HttpClient, HttpHeaders, HttpParams } from '@angular/common/http';
import { Observable } from 'rxjs';
import { CheckInDtoResponse } from '../../models/CheckIn-model';
import { BookingCancellationDtoRequest, BookingCancellationDtoResponse, BookingDtoResponse } from '../../models/Booking-model';

@Injectable({
  providedIn: 'root'
})
export class CheckInService {
  private apiUrl = 'http://localhost:8080/api/check-ins';
  private usersUrl = 'http://localhost:8080/api/users';
  private bookingsUrl = 'http://localhost:8080/private/booking';

  constructor(private http: HttpClient) {}

  private getAuthHeaders(): HttpHeaders {
    const token = localStorage.getItem('token') || '';
    return new HttpHeaders({
      'Content-Type': 'application/json',
      'Authorization': `Bearer ${token}`
    });
  }

  getAll(): Observable<CheckInDtoResponse[]> {
    return this.http.get<CheckInDtoResponse[]>(this.apiUrl, { headers: this.getAuthHeaders() });
  }

  getTodayCheckIns(): Observable<BookingDtoResponse[]> {
    return this.http.get<BookingDtoResponse[]>(`${this.bookingsUrl}/today-checkins`, { headers: this.getAuthHeaders() });
  }

  getBookingById(id: number): Observable<BookingDtoResponse> {
    return this.http.get<BookingDtoResponse>(`${this.bookingsUrl}/${id}`, { headers: this.getAuthHeaders() });
  }

  getBookingsByState(state: string): Observable<BookingDtoResponse[]> {
    return this.http.get<BookingDtoResponse[]>(`${this.bookingsUrl}/state/${state}`, { headers: this.getAuthHeaders() });
  }

  createCheckIn(request: any): Observable<any> {
    return this.http.post(this.apiUrl, request, { headers: this.getAuthHeaders() });
  }

  getMyCheckIns(): Observable<CheckInDtoResponse[] > {
    return this.http.get<CheckInDtoResponse[]>(`${this.apiUrl}/my-check-ins`, { headers: this.getAuthHeaders() });
  }

  getAllUsers(): Observable<any[]> {
    return this.http.get<any[]>(this.usersUrl, { headers: this.getAuthHeaders() });
  }

  checkInBooking(bookingId: number, dni?: string): Observable<BookingDtoResponse> {
    let params = new HttpParams();
    if (dni) {
      params = params.set('dni', dni);
    }
    return this.http.patch<BookingDtoResponse>(`${this.bookingsUrl}/${bookingId}/check-in`, {}, { 
      headers: this.getAuthHeaders(),
      params: params 
    });
  }

  createUser(userData: any): Observable<any> {
    return this.http.post(this.usersUrl, userData, { headers: this.getAuthHeaders() });
  }

  assignUserToBooking(bookingId: number, userId: number): Observable<BookingDtoResponse> {
    return this.http.patch<BookingDtoResponse>(`${this.bookingsUrl}/${bookingId}/assign-user/${userId}`, {}, { headers: this.getAuthHeaders() });
  }

  interruptStay(request: BookingCancellationDtoRequest): Observable<BookingCancellationDtoResponse> {
    return this.http.post<BookingCancellationDtoResponse>(`${this.bookingsUrl}/interrupt`, request, { headers: this.getAuthHeaders() });
  }

  // --- NUEVO MÃ‰TODO PARA CHECK-IN POR QR ---
  checkInByQr(qrCode: string): Observable<BookingDtoResponse> {
    const params = new HttpParams().set('qrCode', qrCode);
    return this.http.post<BookingDtoResponse>(`${this.bookingsUrl}/check-in/qr`, {}, {
      headers: this.getAuthHeaders(),
      params: params
    });
  }
}
