import { inject, Injectable } from '@angular/core';
import { HttpClient } from '@angular/common/http';
import { Observable } from 'rxjs';

import {
  EmployeeDtoResponse,
  EmployeeDtoRequest,
  EmployeeCreateUnifiedDTO,
  ShiftType
} from '../../models/Employee-model';

@Injectable({
  providedIn: 'root'
})
export class EmployeeService {

  private http = inject(HttpClient);

  private readonly apiUrl ='http://localhost:8080/private/employee';

  // Obtener todos los empleados
  getAll(): Observable<EmployeeDtoResponse[]> {
    return this.http.get<EmployeeDtoResponse[]>(this.apiUrl,
      { withCredentials: true });
  }

  // Obtener un empleado por ID
  getById(id: number): Observable<EmployeeDtoResponse> {
    return this.http.get<EmployeeDtoResponse>(`${this.apiUrl}/${id}`,
      { withCredentials: true });
  }

  // Crear un empleado con sus datos de usuario
  create( employee: EmployeeCreateUnifiedDTO): Observable<EmployeeDtoResponse> {

    const formData = new FormData();

    Object.entries(employee).forEach(([key, value]) => {
      if (value !== undefined && value !== null) {
        if (value instanceof File) {
          formData.append(key, value);
        } else {
          formData.append(key, String(value));
        }
      }
    });

    return this.http.post<EmployeeDtoResponse>(this.apiUrl, formData,
      { withCredentials: true });
  }

  // Actualizar los datos laborales de un empleado
  update( id: number, employee: EmployeeDtoRequest): Observable<EmployeeDtoResponse> {
    return this.http.put<EmployeeDtoResponse>(`${this.apiUrl}/${id}`, employee,
      { withCredentials: true });
  }

  // Cambiar el turno de un empleado
  updateShift( id: number, nuevoShift: ShiftType): Observable<EmployeeDtoResponse> {
    return this.http.patch<EmployeeDtoResponse>(`${this.apiUrl}/${id}/shift`, null,
      {
        params: { nuevoShift },
        withCredentials: true
      });
  }

  // Eliminar el perfil laboral de un empleado
  delete(id: number): Observable<void> {
    return this.http.delete<void>(`${this.apiUrl}/${id}`,
      { withCredentials: true });
  }
}


import { inject, Injectable } from '@angular/core';
import { HttpClient } from '@angular/common/http';
import { Observable } from 'rxjs';

import {
  ItemDtoRequest,
  ItemDtoResponse
} from '../../models/Item-model';

@Injectable({
  providedIn: 'root'
})
export class ItemService {

  private http = inject(HttpClient);

  private apiUrl ='http://localhost:8080/private/items';

  //Obtiene todos los productos y servicios registrados.
  getAll(): Observable<ItemDtoResponse[]> {
    return this.http.get<ItemDtoResponse[]>(this.apiUrl,
      { withCredentials: true });
  }

  //Obtiene un producto o servicio especÃ­fico
  getById(id: number): Observable<ItemDtoResponse> {
    return this.http.get<ItemDtoResponse>(`${this.apiUrl}/${id}`,
      { withCredentials: true });
  }

  //Crear items
  createItem( request: ItemDtoRequest, image?: File): Observable<ItemDtoResponse> {

    const formData = new FormData(); //Crea contenedor para enviar datos mediante multipart/form-data, ya que tenemos datos binarios(imagen) y datos en formato json

    formData.append('item', new Blob(
        [JSON.stringify(request)],
        { type: 'application/json' }
      )
    );

    if (image) { //Agrega la imagen solamente si se proporcionÃ³ una
      formData.append('file', image);
    }

    return this.http.post<ItemDtoResponse>(this.apiUrl, formData,
      { withCredentials: true });
  }


//Actualizar item (similar a crear item pero necesita un identificador)
  updateItem(id: number, request: ItemDtoRequest, image?: File): Observable<ItemDtoResponse> {

    const formData = new FormData();

    formData.append('item', new Blob(
        [JSON.stringify(request)],
        { type: 'application/json' }
      )
    );

    if (image) {
      formData.append('file', image);
    }

    return this.http.put<ItemDtoResponse>(`${this.apiUrl}/${id}`, formData,
      { withCredentials: true });
  }

  //Elimina un producto o servicio mediante su identificador
  deleteItem(id: number): Observable<void> {
    return this.http.delete<void>(`${this.apiUrl}/${id}`,
      { withCredentials: true });
  }

  //Obtiene la imagen de un producto
  getItemImage(id: number): Observable<Blob> {
    return this.http.get(`${this.apiUrl}/${id}/image`, {
        responseType: 'blob',
        withCredentials: true
      });
  }

  deleteItemImage(id: number): Observable<ItemDtoResponse> {
  return this.http.delete<ItemDtoResponse>(`${this.apiUrl}/${id}/image`);
}

}

import { Observable, map } from 'rxjs';
import { AccountService } from './account.service';
import { PaymentDTOResponse } from '../../models/Payment-model';
import { AccountDTOResponse } from '../../models/Account-model';

@Injectable({
  providedIn: 'root'
})
export class PaymentService {

  constructor(private accountService: AccountService) {}

  getAllPayments(): Observable<PaymentDTOResponse[]> {
    return this.accountService.getAllAccounts().pipe(
      map((accounts: AccountDTOResponse[]) => {
        const allPayments: PaymentDTOResponse[] = [];
        accounts.forEach((account: AccountDTOResponse) => {
          if (account.payments) {
            account.payments.forEach((payment: PaymentDTOResponse) => {
              allPayments.push(payment);
            });
          }
        });
        return allPayments;
      })
    );
  }
}
import { inject, Injectable } from '@angular/core';
import { HttpClient } from '@angular/common/http';
import { Observable, Subject, tap } from 'rxjs';

import {
  RoomAttentionDtoRequest,
  RoomAttentionDtoResponse
} from '../../models/RoomAttention-model';

@Injectable({
  providedIn: 'root'
})
export class RoomAttentionService {

  private http = inject(HttpClient);
  private apiUrl ='http://localhost:8080/private/room-attentions';

  // --- NUEVO: Canal reactivo para notificar cambios ---
  private attentionUpdatedSource = new Subject<void>();
  attentionUpdated$ = this.attentionUpdatedSource.asObservable();

  // Obtiene TODAS las atenciones del sistema
  getAll(): Observable<RoomAttentionDtoResponse[]> {
    return this.http.get<RoomAttentionDtoResponse[]>(this.apiUrl,
      { withCredentials: true });
  }

  // Obtiene todas las atenciones para una reserva especÃ­fica
  getByBooking(bookingId: number): Observable<RoomAttentionDtoResponse[]> {
    return this.http.get<RoomAttentionDtoResponse[]>(`${this.apiUrl}/booking/${bookingId}`,
      { withCredentials: true });
  }
  
  // Registra un consumo y notifica el cambio
  addAttention(request: RoomAttentionDtoRequest): Observable<RoomAttentionDtoResponse> {
    return this.http.post<RoomAttentionDtoResponse>(this.apiUrl, request,
      { withCredentials: true }).pipe(
        tap(() => this.attentionUpdatedSource.next()) // <-- Notifica actualizaciÃ³n
      );
  }

  // Elimina una atenciÃ³n y notifica el cambio
  deleteAttention(id: number): Observable<void> {
    return this.http.delete<void>(`${this.apiUrl}/${id}`,
      { withCredentials: true }).pipe(
        tap(() => this.attentionUpdatedSource.next()) // <-- Notifica actualizaciÃ³n
      );
  }

  // Marca como entregado y notifica el cambio
  markAsDelivered(id: number): Observable<RoomAttentionDtoResponse> {
    return this.http.patch<RoomAttentionDtoResponse>(`${this.apiUrl}/${id}/deliver`, {},
      { withCredentials: true }).pipe(
        tap(() => this.attentionUpdatedSource.next()) // <-- Notifica actualizaciÃ³n
      );
  }
}
import { inject, Injectable } from '@angular/core';
import { HttpClient } from '@angular/common/http';
import { Observable } from 'rxjs';

import {
  RoomTypeDTORequest,
  RoomTypeDTOResponse
} from '../../models/RoomType-model';

@Injectable({
  providedIn: 'root'
})
export class RoomTypeService {

  private http = inject(HttpClient);

  private apiUrl = 'http://localhost:8080/private/room-type';

  // Obtener todos los tipos de habitaciÃ³n
  getAll(): Observable<RoomTypeDTOResponse[]> {
    return this.http.get<RoomTypeDTOResponse[]>(this.apiUrl,
      { withCredentials: true });
  }

  // Obtener un tipo de habitaciÃ³n por ID
  getById(id: number): Observable<RoomTypeDTOResponse> {
    return this.http.get<RoomTypeDTOResponse>(`${this.apiUrl}/${id}`,
      { withCredentials: true });
  }

  // Crear un tipo de habitaciÃ³n
  createRoomType(request: RoomTypeDTORequest): Observable<RoomTypeDTOResponse> {
    return this.http.post<RoomTypeDTOResponse>(this.apiUrl,request,
      { withCredentials: true });
  }

  // Actualizar un tipo de habitaciÃ³n
  updateRoomType(id: number, request: RoomTypeDTORequest): Observable<RoomTypeDTOResponse> {
    return this.http.put<RoomTypeDTOResponse>(`${this.apiUrl}/${id}`,request,
      { withCredentials: true });
  }

  // Eliminar un tipo de habitaciÃ³n
  deleteRoomType(id: number): Observable<void> {
    return this.http.delete<void>(`${this.apiUrl}/${id}`,
      { withCredentials: true });
  }

}

import { inject, Injectable } from '@angular/core';
import { HttpClient } from '@angular/common/http';
import { Observable } from 'rxjs';

import { RoomDtoResponse } from '../../models/Room-model';
import { RoomDTORequest } from '../../models/Room-model';
import { RoomUpdateDTORequest } from '../../models/Room-model';

@Injectable({
  providedIn: 'root'
})
export class RoomService {

  private readonly http = inject(HttpClient);

  private readonly apiUrl = 'http://localhost:8080/private/room';

  // Obtener todas las habitaciones
  getAll(): Observable<RoomDtoResponse[]> {
    return this.http.get<RoomDtoResponse[]>(
      this.apiUrl,
      { withCredentials: true }
    );
  }

  // Obtener una habitaciÃ³n por ID
  getById(id: number): Observable<RoomDtoResponse> {
    return this.http.get<RoomDtoResponse>(
      `${this.apiUrl}/${id}`,
      { withCredentials: true }
    );
  }

  // Obtener habitaciones disponibles
  getAvailableRooms(): Observable<RoomDtoResponse[]> {
    return this.http.get<RoomDtoResponse[]>(
      `${this.apiUrl}/available`,
      { withCredentials: true }
    );
  }

  // Crear una habitaciÃ³n con imÃ¡genes opcionales
  createRoom(
    request: RoomDTORequest,
    images: File[] = []
  ): Observable<RoomDtoResponse> {

    const formData = new FormData();

    formData.append('number', request.number.toString());
    formData.append('roomTypeId', request.roomTypeId.toString());
    formData.append('state', request.state ?? 'AVAILABLE');

    images.forEach(file => {
      formData.append('images', file, file.name);
    });

    return this.http.post<RoomDtoResponse>(
      this.apiUrl,
      formData,
      { withCredentials: true }
    );
  }

  // Actualizar una habitaciÃ³n
  updateRoom(
    id: number,
    request: RoomUpdateDTORequest
  ): Observable<RoomDtoResponse> {

    return this.http.put<RoomDtoResponse>(
      `${this.apiUrl}/${id}`,
      request,
      { withCredentials: true }
    );
  }

  // Eliminar una habitaciÃ³n
  deleteRoom(id: number): Observable<void> {
    return this.http.delete<void>(
      `${this.apiUrl}/${id}`,
      { withCredentials: true }
    );
  }

  // Cambiar una habitaciÃ³n a mantenimiento
  putInMaintenance(id: number): Observable<RoomDtoResponse> {
    return this.http.patch<RoomDtoResponse>(
      `${this.apiUrl}/${id}/maintenance`,
      {},
      { withCredentials: true }
    );
  }

  // Obtener cantidad total de habitaciones
  getRoomCount(): Observable<number> {
    return this.http.get<number>(
      `${this.apiUrl}/count`,
      { withCredentials: true }
    );
  }

  // Agregar imÃ¡genes a una habitaciÃ³n existente
  addImages(
    id: number,
    images: File[]
  ): Observable<RoomDtoResponse> {

    const formData = new FormData();

    images.forEach(file => {
      formData.append('images', file, file.name);
    });

    return this.http.post<RoomDtoResponse>(
      `${this.apiUrl}/${id}/images`,
      formData,
      { withCredentials: true }
    );
  }

  // Obtener una imagen especÃ­fica
  getRoomImage(
    id: number,
    index: number
  ): Observable<Blob> {

    return this.http.get(
      `${this.apiUrl}/${id}/images/${index}`,
      {
        responseType: 'blob',
        withCredentials: true
      }
    );
  }

  deleteRoomImage(roomId: number, index: number): Observable<RoomDtoResponse> {
    return this.http.delete<RoomDtoResponse>(
      `${this.apiUrl}/${roomId}/images/${index}`,
      { withCredentials: true } 
    );
  }

}


import { Injectable } from '@angular/core';
import { HttpClient, HttpHeaders } from '@angular/common/http';
import { Observable } from 'rxjs';
import { ChangePasswordDtoRequest } from '../../models/ChangePassword-model';
import { UserDtoRequestCreation,UserDtoResponse, UserDtoRequest } from '../../models/User-model';


@Injectable({
  providedIn: 'root'
})
export class UserService {
  private apiUrl = 'http://localhost:8080/private/user';

  constructor(private http: HttpClient) {}

  // POST /private/user/change-password
  changePassword(request: ChangePasswordDtoRequest): Observable<void> {
    return this.http.post<void>(`${this.apiUrl}/change-password`, request, { withCredentials: true });
  }

  // POST /private/user (CreaciÃ³n de usuario con rol y foto de perfil opcional)
  createUser(request: UserDtoRequestCreation): Observable<UserDtoResponse> {
    const formData = new FormData();

    // Recorremos todas las propiedades del objeto y las agregamos al FormData
    Object.keys(request).forEach(key => {
      const value = (request as any)[key];
      
      // Verificamos que el valor no sea nulo o indefinido antes de agregarlo
      if (value !== null && value !== undefined) {
        // Si es el archivo de la foto, lo agregamos directamente como File
        if (key === 'profilePictureFile' && value instanceof File) {
          formData.append(key, value, value.name);
        } else {
          // Para los demÃ¡s campos de texto, nÃºmeros o enums, los convertimos a string
          formData.append(key, value.toString());
        }
      }
    });

    // Nota: HttpClient detecta automÃ¡ticamente el FormData y establece el Content-Type como multipart/form-data
    return this.http.post<UserDtoResponse>(this.apiUrl, formData, { withCredentials: true });
  }

  // GET /private/user/{id}
  getById(id: number): Observable<UserDtoResponse> {
    return this.http.get<UserDtoResponse>(`${this.apiUrl}/${id}`, { withCredentials: true });
  }

  // GET /private/user/dni/{dni}
  getByDni(dni: string): Observable<UserDtoResponse> {
    return this.http.get<UserDtoResponse>(`${this.apiUrl}/dni/${dni}`, { withCredentials: true });
  }

  // GET /private/user (Listar todos los usuarios)
  getAll(): Observable<UserDtoResponse[]> {
    return this.http.get<UserDtoResponse[]>(this.apiUrl, { withCredentials: true });
  }

  // PUT /private/user/{id}
  updateUser(id: number, request: UserDtoRequest): Observable<UserDtoResponse> {
    return this.http.put<UserDtoResponse>(`${this.apiUrl}/${id}`, request, { withCredentials: true });
  }

  // DELETE /private/user/{id}
  deleteUser(id: number): Observable<void> {
    return this.http.delete<void>(`${this.apiUrl}/${id}`, { withCredentials: true });
  }

  getByRole(roleName: string): Observable<UserDtoResponse[]> {
  return this.http.get<UserDtoResponse[]>(`${this.apiUrl}/role/${roleName}`, { headers: this.getHeaders() });
}

private getHeaders(): HttpHeaders {
  const token = localStorage.getItem('token'); 
  return new HttpHeaders({
    'Content-Type': 'application/json',
    'Authorization': token ? `Bearer ${token}` : ''
  });
}

// GET /private/user/{id}/profile-picture
  getProfilePicture(id: number): Observable<Blob> {
  return this.http.get(`${this.apiUrl}/${id}/profile-picture`, {
    headers: this.getHeaders(), // Asegura que use las cabeceras con el Token
    responseType: 'blob'
  });
}

 getUserByUsername(username: string): Observable<any> {
    return this.http.get<any>(`http://localhost:8080/public/auth/by-username/${username}`);
  }

updateProfilePicture(id: number, formData: FormData): Observable<any> {
    return this.http.post<any>(`${this.apiUrl}/${id}/profile-picture`, formData, { withCredentials: true });
  }

}

export type AccountState = 'OPEN' | 'PAID' | 'CANCELLED' | 'REFUNDED';

export interface AccountDTOResponse {
  id: number;
  bookingId: number;
  baseAmount: number;
  servicesTotal?: number;          // AÃ±adido para que coincida con el backend
  totalAmount?: number;            // Calculado o mapeado en la vista/backend
  paidAmount: number;
  remainingBalance?: number;       // Calculado o mapeado en la vista/backend
  isPaid: boolean;
  adjustmentPercentage: number;
  state: AccountState;             // AÃ±adido para reflejar el estado financiero (OPEN, PAID, CANCELLED, REFUNDED)
  payments: PaymentDTOResponse[];
  user?: {
    name: string;
    surname: string;
    dni: string;
  };
  roomNumber?: string | number;
  checkInDate?: string;
  checkOutDate?: string;
  items?: {
    description: string;
    quantity: number;
    subtotal: number;
  }[];
}

import { Component, OnInit, OnDestroy, signal, inject } from '@angular/core';
import { CommonModule } from '@angular/common';
import { Router, RouterOutlet, RouterLink, RouterLinkActive } from '@angular/router';
import { Subscription } from 'rxjs'; // <-- NUEVO
import { AuthService } from '../../core/services/auth.service';
import { RoomAttentionService } from '../../core/services/room-attention.service';

@Component({
  selector: 'app-dashboard',
  standalone: true,
  imports: [CommonModule, RouterOutlet, RouterLink, RouterLinkActive],
  templateUrl: './dashboard.component.html',
  styleUrls: ['./dashboard.component.css']
})
export class DashboardComponent implements OnInit, OnDestroy {
  username: string = 'Usuario';
  userRole: string = '';
  userId: number | null = null;
  avatarUrl: string | null = null;

  pendingAttentionsCount = signal<number>(0);
  private attentionSub?: Subscription; // <-- NUEVO

  private router = inject(Router);
  private authService = inject(AuthService);
  private roomAttentionService = inject(RoomAttentionService);

  ngOnInit(): void {
    const token = localStorage.getItem('authToken');
    if (token) {
      try {
        const payload = JSON.parse(atob(token.split('.')[1]));
        this.username = payload.sub || localStorage.getItem('username') || 'Usuario';
        
        const roles = payload.roles || [];
        if (Array.isArray(roles) && roles.length > 0) {
          this.userRole = roles[0].replace(/^ROLE_/i, '').trim();
        }

        const storedId = localStorage.getItem('userId');
        if (storedId) {
          this.userId = Number(storedId);
          this.loadProfilePictureById(this.userId);
        } else if (this.username && this.username !== 'Usuario') {
          this.authService.getUserIdByUsername(this.username).subscribe({
            next: (id) => {
              if (id) {
                this.userId = id;
                localStorage.setItem('userId', id.toString());
                this.loadProfilePictureById(id);
              }
            },
            error: (err) => {
              console.warn('No se pudo obtener el ID del usuario:', err);
            }
          });
        }
      } catch (e) {
        console.error('Error al decodificar el token:', e);
      }
    }

    // Carga inicial de pendientes
    this.loadPendingAttentions();

    // --- NUEVO: Suscribirse a los cambios en tiempo real ---
    this.attentionSub = this.roomAttentionService.attentionUpdated$.subscribe(() => {
      this.loadPendingAttentions();
    });
  }

  ngOnDestroy(): void {
    // Evitar fugas de memoria desuscribiÃ©ndose al destruir el componente
    if (this.attentionSub) {
      this.attentionSub.unsubscribe();
    }
  }

  loadPendingAttentions(): void {
    if (this.userRole.toLowerCase() !== 'guest') {
      this.roomAttentionService.getAll().subscribe({
        next: (data) => {
          const pending = data.filter(item => !item.delivered).length;
          this.pendingAttentionsCount.set(pending);
        },
        error: (err) => {
          console.warn('No se pudieron cargar las notificaciones de servicio al cuarto', err);
        }
      });
    }
  }

  loadProfilePictureById(id: number): void {
    this.avatarUrl = `http://localhost:8080/public/auth/${id}/profile-picture?t=${new Date().getTime()}`;
  }

  onAvatarError(): void {
    this.avatarUrl = null; 
  }

  logout(): void {
    localStorage.clear();
    this.router.navigate(['/']);
  }
}

          <img src="https://images.unsplash.com/photo-1540555700478-4be289fbecef?auto=format&fit=crop&w=800&q=80" alt="Spa y Relax" class="amenity-img" />
        </div>
        <div class="amenity-content">
          <h3>Ãreas de Bienestar y Spa</h3>
          <p class="subtitle">Disfruta de piscinas climatizadas, masajes profesionales y zonas de relajaciÃ³n exclusivas para huÃ©spedes.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  </section>

  <!-- ================= SECCIÃ“N 3: COMENTARIOS (CARRUSEL CON FOTO) ================= -->
  <section id="testimonials" class="testimonials-section glass-card">
    <div class="section-header text-center mb-4">
      <h2><i class="bi bi-chat-square-quote me-2"></i> Lo que dicen nuestros HuÃ©spedes</h2>
      <p class="subtitle">Experiencias y opiniones compartidas por nuestra comunidad.</p>
    </div>

    @if (fiveStarComments().length > 0) {
      <div class="carousel-container">
        @let current = fiveStarComments()[currentCommentIndex()];
        
        <div class="carousel-card glass-subcard">
          <div class="comment-rating mb-3">
            @for (star of [1, 2, 3, 4, 5]; track star) {
              <i class="bi bi-star-fill text-warning"></i>
            }
          </div>
          
          <p class="carousel-text">"{{ current['comment'] ?? current['content'] ?? 'Excelente estadÃ­a' }}"</p>
          
          <!-- Datos y foto del autor del comentario -->
          <div class="carousel-author d-flex align-items-center justify-content-center gap-3 mt-4">
            @if (current['authorAvatar']) {
              <img [src]="current['authorAvatar']" (error)="onCommentAvatarError($event)" alt="Avatar Autor" class="comment-author-avatar">
            } @else {
              <div class="comment-author-avatar-default">
                <i class="bi bi-person-fill"></i>
              </div>
            }
            <div class="d-flex flex-column text-start">
              <strong>{{ current['username'] ?? current['name'] ?? 'HuÃ©sped Verificado' }}</strong>
              <span class="subtitle">HuÃ©sped Ultimate Hotel</span>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div class="carousel-controls mt-4">
          <button type="button" class="btn-secondary-action" (click)="prevComment()">
            <i class="bi bi-chevron-left me-1"></i> Anterior
          </button>
          <span class="subtitle">{{ currentCommentIndex() + 1 }} de {{ fiveStarComments().length }}</span>
          <button type="button" class="btn-secondary-action" (click)="nextComment()">
            Siguiente <i class="bi bi-chevron-right ms-1"></i>
          </button>

    } @else {

import { Component, ChangeDetectionStrategy, signal, inject, OnInit } from '@angular/core';
import { CommonModule } from '@angular/common';
import { Router, RouterLink } from '@angular/router';
import { FormsModule } from '@angular/forms';
import { forkJoin, of } from 'rxjs';
import { catchError, map } from 'rxjs/operators';
import { CommentService } from '../../core/services/comment.service';
import { CommentDtoResponse } from '../../models/Comment-model';
import { UserService } from '../../core/services/user.service';

@Component({
  selector: 'app-landing',
  standalone: true,
  imports: [CommonModule, RouterLink, FormsModule],
  templateUrl: './landing.component.html',
  styleUrls: ['./landing.component.css'],
  changeDetection: ChangeDetectionStrategy.OnPush
})
export class LandingComponent implements OnInit {
  
  private commentService = inject(CommentService);
  private router = inject(Router);
  private userService = inject(UserService);

  // --- Estados de AutenticaciÃ³n para el Navbar ---
  isLoggedIn = signal<boolean>(false);
  username = signal<string>('Usuario');
  userRole = signal<string>('');
  userId = signal<number | null>(null);
  avatarUrl = signal<string | null>(null);

  // --- Estado del Carrusel de Comentarios ---
  fiveStarComments = signal<any[]>([]);
  currentCommentIndex = signal<number>(0);

  ngOnInit(): void {
    this.checkAuthStatus();
    this.loadFiveStarComments();
  }

  /**
   * Verifica el estado de autenticaciÃ³n del usuario leyendo el localStorage y el token JWT
   */
  checkAuthStatus(): void {
    const token = localStorage.getItem('authToken');
    if (token) {
      try {
        const payload = JSON.parse(atob(token.split('.')[1]));
        this.isLoggedIn.set(true);
        this.username.set(payload.sub || localStorage.getItem('username') || 'Usuario');
        
        const roles = payload.roles || [];
        if (Array.isArray(roles) && roles.length > 0) {
          this.userRole.set(roles[0].replace(/^ROLE_/i, '').trim());
        }

        const storedId = localStorage.getItem('userId');
        if (storedId) {
          this.userId.set(Number(storedId));
          this.loadProfilePictureById(Number(storedId));
        }
      } catch (e) {
        console.error('Error al decodificar el token en la landing:', e);
        this.logout();
      }
    }
  }

  loadProfilePictureById(id: number): void {
    this.avatarUrl.set(`http://localhost:8080/public/auth/${id}/profile-picture?t=${new Date().getTime()}`);
  }

  onAvatarError(): void {
    this.avatarUrl.set(null);
  }

  logout(): void {
    localStorage.clear();
    this.isLoggedIn.set(false);
    this.username.set('Usuario');
    this.userRole.set('');
    this.userId.set(null);
    this.avatarUrl.set(null);
    this.router.navigate(['/']);
  }

  /**
   * Carga los comentarios, filtra los de 5 estrellas y busca el ID del usuario por su username para su foto
   */
  loadFiveStarComments(): void {
    // Llamar al endpoint pÃºblico que solo trae showable = true
    this.commentService.getVisibleComments().subscribe({
      next: (comments) => {
        // Filtramos los de 5 estrellas (o usamos todos si no hay)
        const filtered = comments.filter((c: any) => c.rating === 5 || c.puntuacion === 5);
        const listToProcess = filtered.length > 0 ? filtered : comments;

        // Consultamos en paralelo el ID de usuario usando el username de cada comentario
        const requests = listToProcess.map((c: any) => {
          if (!c.username) {
            return of({ ...c, authorAvatar: null });
          }
          return this.userService.getUserByUsername(c.username).pipe(
            map((user: any) => {
              return {
                ...c,
                authorAvatar: c.username ? `http://localhost:8080/public/users/username/${c.username}/profile-picture?t=${new Date().getTime()}` : null
              };
            }),
            catchError(() => of({ ...c, authorAvatar: null }))
          );
        });

        forkJoin(requests).subscribe({
          next: (processedComments) => {
            this.fiveStarComments.set(processedComments);
          },
          error: (err) => {
            console.error('Error al procesar avatares de comentarios:', err);
            this.fiveStarComments.set(listToProcess);
          }
        });
      },
      error: (err) => {
        console.error('Error al cargar comentarios visibles:', err);
      }
    });
  }

  onCommentAvatarError(event: Event): void {
    const target = event.target as HTMLElement;
    target.style.display = 'none';
  }

  // --- Controles del Carrusel ---
  nextComment(): void {
    const total = this.fiveStarComments().length;
    if (total === 0) return;
    this.currentCommentIndex.update(index => (index + 1) % total);
  }

  prevComment(): void {
    const total = this.fiveStarComments().length;
    if (total === 0) return;
    this.currentCommentIndex.update(index => (index - 1 + total) % total);
  }

  // --- Manejo de redirecciÃ³n para reservas con validaciÃ³n de sesiÃ³n ---
  onReservarClick(event: Event): void {
    event.preventDefault();
    const token = localStorage.getItem('authToken');

    if (!token) {
      alert('Debes iniciar sesiÃ³n en el sistema antes de realizar una reserva.');
      this.router.navigate(['/login']);
    } else {
      this.router.navigate(['/dashboard/availability']); 
    }
  }

}

import { Component, inject } from '@angular/core';
import { CommonModule } from '@angular/common';
import { FormBuilder, FormGroup, Validators, ReactiveFormsModule } from '@angular/forms';
import { Router } from '@angular/router';
import { AuthService } from '../../core/services/auth.service';
import { finalize } from 'rxjs';
import { ChangeDetectorRef } from '@angular/core';
import { RouterLink } from '@angular/router';

@Component({
  selector: 'app-login',
  standalone: true,
  imports: [CommonModule, ReactiveFormsModule, RouterLink],
  templateUrl: './login.component.html',
  styleUrls: ['./login.component.css']
})
export class LoginComponent {
  
  private authService = inject(AuthService);
  private fb = inject(FormBuilder);
  private router = inject(Router);
  private cdr = inject(ChangeDetectorRef);

 
  errorMessage: string = '';
  loading: boolean = false;

  loginForm: FormGroup = this.fb.group({
      username: ['', [Validators.required]],
      password: ['', [Validators.required]]
    });

  onSubmit(): void {
    if (this.loginForm.invalid) return;

    this.loading = true;
    this.errorMessage = '';

    const loginData = this.loginForm.value;

    this.authService.login(loginData).pipe(
      finalize(() => {
        this.loading = false;
        this.cdr.markForCheck();
      })
    ).subscribe({
      next: (response: any) => {
    // Guardar el token principal
    localStorage.setItem('authToken', response.token);

    // Decodificar el JWT para extraer el ID, username, roles, nombre y apellido
    try {
      const payloadBase64 = response.token.split('.')[1];
      const decodedJson = atob(payloadBase64);
      const decodedToken = JSON.parse(decodedJson);

      // 1. Extraer y guardar el ID del usuario desde la respuesta
      const userId = response.id;
      if (userId) {
        localStorage.setItem('userId', userId.toString());
      }

      // 2. Guardar el username desde el formulario como respaldo
      const username = loginData.username;
      localStorage.setItem('username', username);

      // 3. Guardar roles, nombre y apellido desde el token JWT
      const roles = decodedToken.roles || [];
      localStorage.setItem('role', JSON.stringify(roles));

      if (decodedToken.name) {
        localStorage.setItem('name', decodedToken.name);
      }
      if (decodedToken.surname) {
        localStorage.setItem('surname', decodedToken.surname);
      }
    } catch (e) {
      console.error('Error al decodificar el token:', e);
      localStorage.setItem('username', loginData.username);
    }

    this.router.navigate(['/dashboard']);
  },
      error: (err) => {
        this.errorMessage = err.error?.mensaje || 'Usuario o contraseÃ±a incorrectos.';
        this.cdr.markForCheck();
        console.error('Error de login:', err);
      }
    });
  }
}

import { Component, ChangeDetectorRef, inject } from '@angular/core';
import { CommonModule } from '@angular/common';
import { FormsModule } from '@angular/forms';
import { Router, RouterLink } from '@angular/router';
import { AuthService } from '../../core/services/auth.service';
import { finalize } from 'rxjs';

@Component({
  selector: 'app-register',
  standalone: true,
  imports: [CommonModule, FormsModule, RouterLink],
  templateUrl: './register.component.html',
  styleUrls: ['./register.component.css']
})
export class RegisterComponent {
  private authService = inject(AuthService);
  private router = inject(Router);
  private cdr = inject(ChangeDetectorRef);

  errorMessage: string = '';
  loading: boolean = false;

  formData = {
    username: '',
    password: '',
    name: '',
    surname: '',
    dni: '',
    gender: '',
    email: '',
    phoneNumber: '',
    address: '',
    birthDay: '',
    profilePictureFile: undefined as File | undefined
  };

  onFileSelected(event: any): void {
    const file: File = event.target.files[0];
    if (file) {
      this.formData.profilePictureFile = file;
    }
  }

  onSubmit(): void {
    console.log('--- Intentando registrar ---');
    console.log('Valores del formulario:', this.formData);

    this.loading = true;
    this.errorMessage = '';

    // Aseguramos que birthDay sea un string vacÃ­o si no se selecciona para cumplir con el tipo
    const payload = {
      username: this.formData.username,
      password: this.formData.password,
      name: this.formData.name,
      surname: this.formData.surname,
      dni: this.formData.dni,
      gender: this.formData.gender || '',
      email: this.formData.email,
      phoneNumber: this.formData.phoneNumber,
      address: this.formData.address || '',
      birthDay: this.formData.birthDay ? this.formData.birthDay : '',
      profilePictureFile: this.formData.profilePictureFile
    };

    this.authService.register(payload as any).pipe(
      finalize(() => {
        this.loading = false;
        this.cdr.markForCheck();
      })
    ).subscribe({
      next: (response: any) => {
        console.log('Registro exitoso, respuesta:', response);
        const token = response.token;
        if (token) {
          localStorage.setItem('authToken', token);
        }

        const username = this.formData.username;
        localStorage.setItem('username', username);

        try {
          if (token) {
            const payloadBase64 = token.split('.')[1];
            const decodedJson = atob(payloadBase64);
            const decodedToken = JSON.parse(decodedJson);

            const roles = decodedToken.roles || [];
            localStorage.setItem('role', JSON.stringify(roles));

            if (decodedToken.name) localStorage.setItem('name', decodedToken.name);
            if (decodedToken.surname) localStorage.setItem('surname', decodedToken.surname);
          }
        } catch (e) {
          console.error('Error al decodificar el token en registro:', e);
        }

        this.authService.getUserIdByUsername(username).subscribe({
          next: (userId) => {
            if (userId) {
              localStorage.setItem('userId', userId.toString());
            }
            this.router.navigate(['/home']);
          },
          error: (err) => {
            console.warn('No se pudo obtener el ID tras el registro, redirigiendo de todas formas...', err);
            this.router.navigate(['/home']);
          }
        });
      },
      error: (err) => {
        this.errorMessage = err.error?.message || err.error?.mensaje || 'OcurriÃ³ un error al registrarse. Verifique los datos o si el username/email/DNI ya existen.';
        this.cdr.markForCheck();
        console.error('Error de registro en backend:', err);
      }
    });
  }
}
<router-outlet></router-outlet>
import { Component } from '@angular/core';
import { RouterOutlet } from '@angular/router';

@Component({
  selector: 'app-root',
  standalone: true,
  imports: [RouterOutlet],
  templateUrl: './app.component.html',
  styleUrl: './app.component.css'
})
export class AppComponent {
  title = 'FrontHotel';
}

import { provideHttpClient, withInterceptors } from '@angular/common/http';

import { routes } from './app.routes';
import { authInterceptor } from '../app/core/interceptors/auth';
export const appConfig: ApplicationConfig = {
  providers: [
    provideRouter(routes),
    // Agregamos el cliente HTTP con tu interceptor sin tocar la detecciÃ³n de zonas
    provideHttpClient(withInterceptors([authInterceptor]))
  ]
};
